The crossover predicted by theory appears at ng~100 on PCAM, 20-50 on ISIC, and 250-500 on NIH-CXR; weak labels above the crossover degrade AUC by up to -0.10. The location is architecture-invariant for four of five pretrained architectures, and a within-family DenseNet sweep (2.5x parameters, identical pretraining) supports the view that the labeler, not the student, is the dominant constraint. The calibration in turn produces a decision rule operable from 10-20 gold labels: compare gold-only AUC to VLM accuracy on the user's gold set. A structured-vs-random noise sign flip on NIH-CXR shows that the rate-only formulation of the bound is incomplete and identifies a concrete refinement (label-space projection) that future benchmarks can be designed to test.

</details>


### [74] [Self-Supervised Contrastive Learning for Cardiac MR Sequence Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24789)
*Yuli Wang,Hyewon Jung,Dongshen Peng,Yuwei Dai,Jing Wu,Haoyue Guan,Yoko Kato,Zhicheng Jiao,Yu Sun,Ihab Kamel,Joao Lima,Cheng Ting Lin,Harrison Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了Vision Transformer (ViT) 在心脏磁共振图像上的适配问题。作者发现预训练的ViT特征无法有效迁移到心脏MR领域，因此提出了一种基于图像自监督对比学习的适配策略，该方法优于传统监督训练。适配后的ViT模型在外部MR数据集（BraTS和ADNI）上表现出强泛化能力，并在四种常见心脏MR序列上实现了超过0.75的分类AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT模型在通用视觉任务中表现优异，但通常在公共数据集上预训练，缺乏医学影像所需的专业领域知识。现有方法未能有效解决ViT特征向心脏MR图像领域迁移的问题，因此需要探索专门的适配策略来弥补这一领域差距。

Method: 采用基于图像的自监督对比学习作为适配策略，在内部心脏MR数据集上对ViT模型进行领域自适应训练，与传统监督训练方法进行对比，并通过消融实验分析batch size和数据集规模对性能的影响。

Result: 自监督对比学习适配策略优于传统监督训练；适配后模型在外部数据集BraTS和ADNI上表现良好；在四种常见心脏MR序列分类任务上AUC均超过0.75。

Conclusion: 自监督对比学习是ViT模型向医学影像领域迁移的有效策略，能够克服预训练特征领域不匹配的问题，且适配后的模型具有较强的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) models, utilizing self-attention mechanisms, have demonstrated robust generalization capabilities across various vision tasks, including image classification. However, these models, typically pretrained on general public datasets, often lack the specialized domain knowledge necessary for medical imaging applications. In this study, we investigate the adaptation of ViT models, specifically for cardiac magnetic resonance (MR) images, using an in-house dataset. We found that pretrained ViT features do not effectively transfer to the cardiac MR domain. To overcome this limitation, we introduce an adaptation strategy that utilizes image-based self-supervised contrastive learning, demonstrating superior performance compared to traditional supervised training approaches. Moreover, our adapted ViT model exhibits strong generalization to external MR datasets such as BraTS and ADNI. Through ablation studies, we further investigate the impact of batch size and dataset scale on performance. Ultimately, our adapted model achieves classification AUC exceeding 0.75 across the four most common cardiac MR sequences.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient VLMs for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy: Medical Image Generation and Clinical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24792)
*Ojonugwa Oluwafemi Ejiga Peter,Frederick Akor Ejiga,Fahmi Khalifa,Md Mahmudur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双管道PEFT框架，结合Florence-2进行医学视觉问答（VQA）和LoRA+Stable Diffusion 2.1生成隐私保护的合成GI内镜图像，在Kvasir-VQA数据集上取得了优异的VQA和合成图像质量指标，计算成本降低约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GI内镜AI系统面临标注数据稀缺、严格隐私政策和传统微调计算瓶颈三大限制，阻碍了AI模型在临床实践中的可靠部署与规模化应用。

Method: 采用双管道PEFT框架：1) 基于Florene-2视觉语言模型进行VQA，使用PEFT提升可解释性并降低计算成本；2) 采用LoRA结合Stable Diffusion 2.1生成高质量GI合成图像，避免患者隐私泄露。使用Kvasir-VQA数据集进行验证。

Result: VQA任务：ROUGE-1达0.92，ROUGE-L达0.91，BLEU从0.08提升至0.24。合成图像：rank-4 LoRA最优，Fidelity 0.290，Agreement 0.730，FBD 1450，计算成本降低约90%。相比FLUX、MSDM、Kandinsky 2.2，模型在FBD和语义对齐上更优。

Conclusion: 该双管道PEFT框架有效解决了GI内镜AI中VQA和隐私保护数据生成两大问题，提升了临床AI的实用潜力，为医学AI中VQA和合成数据生成提供了稳健解决方案。

Abstract: The major limitations of gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy AI systems arise from a shortage of annotated data, strict privacy policies, and significant bottlenecks in conventional model fine-tuning. Such limitations impede the successful application of sophisticated AI models in clinical practice, particularly affecting the reliability and scalability of diagnosis. In this paper, we present a dual-pipeline PEFT model that addresses two fundamental problems: medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) and the generation of privacy-preserving synthetic data. For clinical VQA, we adopt the Florence-2 vision-language model. Leveraging PEFT enhances model interpretability while substantially reducing the computational cost of training. Simultaneously, we employ Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with Stable Diffusion 2.1 to generate high-quality GI images that enhance training databases without violating patient privacy. This research utilized the Kvasir-VQA dataset. Our Florence-2 VQA model achieved ROUGE-1 of 0.92, ROUGE-L of 0.91, and BLEU score improvements from 0.08 to 0.24. Fine-tuning on private datasets consistently showed better results than fine-tuning on public datasets. The rank-4 LoRA synthesis achieved optimal performance with a fidelity score of 0.290, an agreement score of 0.730, and a Frechet BiomedCLIP Distance (FBD) of 1450, reducing computational costs by almost 90 percent. This framework improves the clinical potential of AI in GI endoscopy. Compared to FLUX, MSDM, and Kandinsky 2.2, our model demonstrates superior FBD and strong semantic alignment. While other models lead in Fidelity or Agreement, our lower FBD indicates better image-text coherence. These results establish our approach as a robust solution for enhancing VQA and synthetic data generation in clinical AI.

</details>


### [76] [DUEL: Adversarial Self-Play for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24794)
*Lin Qiu,Hanqing Zeng,Yao Liu,Bingjun Sun,Guangdeng Liao,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUEL是一个自演进的视觉语言模型后训练框架，通过挑战者与求解器两个策略的对抗互动生成监督信号，无需人工标注或外部奖励模型，利用长度归一化对数似然奖励提升视觉推理和鲁棒判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL优化依赖昂贵的高质量标注，难以扩展；无监督替代方案因弱视觉 grounding 和缺乏可靠验证信号而易陷入有偏解。

Method: 提出DUEL框架：1) 挑战者生成基于图像的真实陈述及其最小扰动的硬负样本对；2) 求解器验证两个陈述与图像的对应关系，促进细粒度视觉判别；3) 引入长度归一化对数似然奖励，提供超越二值结果的优化信号，提升稀疏反馈下的学习稳定性。

Result: DUEL在无需人工标注、外部奖励模型或图像编辑工具的条件下，持续提升了视觉推理能力和鲁棒判别性能。

Conclusion: DUEL验证了通过策略间对抗互动可以自生成有效监督信号，为VLM后训练提供了一种可扩展的无监督优化范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning capability of vision-language models (VLMs). However, RL-based optimization typically depends on costly high-quality annotations that are difficult to scale. Existing unsupervised alternatives may drift toward biased solutions due to weak visual grounding and the lack of reliable verification signals. We propose a self-evolving post-training framework, DUEL, where supervision emerges from adversarial interactions between two policies initialized from the same pretrained VLM. A Challenger generates an image-grounded true claim together with a minimally perturbed hard-negative counterpart, while a Solver verifies both claims against the image, encouraging fine-grained visual discrimination under near-neighbor semantics. To stabilize optimization, we introduce a length-normalized log-likelihood reward that preserves informative optimization signals beyond binary outcome supervision and improves learning stability under sparse feedback. Experiments show that DUEL consistently improves visual reasoning and robust discrimination without additional human annotations, external reward models, or image editing tools.

</details>


### [77] [Divide-and-Conquer Inference for Large-Scale Visual Recognition with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24799)
*Zhipeng Ye,Jiaqi Huang,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Yikang Duan,Dawei Wang,Xihang Zhou,Qian Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI)策略，通过递归分解大规模分类任务为局部子问题并结合动态剪枝机制，有效缓解了多模态大模型在长序列识别中的性能崩塌问题，使轻量级开源模型无需微调即可媲美前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在处理大规模图像分类时，随着标签空间扩大，性能显著下降（即长序列识别中的性能崩塌现象），其根本原因是高信息熵与注意力机制的稀释和衰减之间的冲突，导致模型在超长提示中难以维持足够信噪比。

Method: 提出Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI)测试时扩展策略：递归地将全局分类任务分解为多个更简单的局部子问题，并通过动态剪枝机制压缩搜索空间，从而改善局部信噪比，缓解长序列推理中的注意力权重稀释问题。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-21K等基准上的实验表明，DCI持续提升分类精度，使轻量级开源模型无需任何额外训练或微调即可媲美甚至超越前沿闭源模型，同时相较于传统自注意力的二次复杂度，实现了更优的计算扩展行为并大幅加速推理。

Conclusion: DCI作为一种模型无关的即插即用范式，为大规模场景下扩展多模态大模型推理精度提供了一种高效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across a wide range of vision language tasks. However, when applied to large scale image classification, their performance degrades significantly as the label space expands a phenomenon we define as Performance Collapse in Long Sequence Recognition. Through an information theoretic analysis, we reveal that this collapse stems from a fundamental conflict between the escalating information entropy and the prominent attention dilution and decay within attention mechanisms, which impairs the model's ability to maintain a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio when processing extremely long prompts. To mitigate this, we propose Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI), a novel test-time scaling strategy for visual recognition with MLLMs. DCI recursively decomposes complex global classification tasks into multiple simpler, localized subproblems and employs a dynamic pruning mechanism to compress the search space. This method effectively improves the local signal to noise ratio and model accuracy by mitigating the inherent weight dilution issues in long-sequence inference. Moreover, while traditional self-attention incurs a prohibitive quadratic computational complexity, DCI achieves more favorable scaling behavior and substantially accelerates inference in large scale classification scenarios. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-21K demonstrate that DCI consistently improves classification accuracy. This enables lightweight open-source models to rival or even surpass frontier closed-source giants without any additional training or fine-tuning. As a model-agnostic, plug-and-play paradigm, DCI offers an efficient approach for scaling the inferential precision of MLLMs in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [78] [Fishbone: From One 3D Asset to a Million Controllable Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24805)
*Yumeng He,Xiaoying Wang,Peihao Li,Yanjia Huang,Joe Masterjohn,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Yin Yang,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fishbone提出了一种统一的"肋-脊"表征方法，能够对任意3D网格进行可控参数化变形、降维动力学仿真和动画，并构建了Fishbone-136K大规模数据集，在可控3D生成、机器人数据增强、交互编辑等多个任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模可控3D资产生成因手工建模和蒙皮成本高昂而极具挑战。现有方法要么依赖稀疏用户输入，要么需要手动设计控制结构和类别特定配置，缺乏统一的通用变形表征。

Method: 受自然生物脊柱和肋骨结构启发，Fishbone首先通过自适应热方法计算测地标量场，提取等值线作为截面肋骨，构建平滑的几何感知脊柱，并利用高斯加权蒙皮将表面顶点与肋-脊结构关联。肋骨控制局部轮廓（厚度、方向、截面变化），脊柱控制全局弯曲、扭转和拉伸。该方法还支持降维仿真和关键帧动画。

Result: 构建了Fishbone-136K数据集（基于Hunyuan3D扩充肋-脊结构），在可控3D生成、基于变形的机器人学习数据增强、交互式网格编辑和智能体生成等任务中展示了有效性、高效性和通用性。

Conclusion: Fishbone作为一种统一的肋-脊表征，为通用形状的可控参数化变形和动画提供了高效解决方案，在多类下游应用中表现出色。

Abstract: Large-scale controllable 3D assets are critical for computer graphics, embodied AI, robotics, and interactive content creation, yet creating diverse 3D assets remains challenging due to the high cost of manual modeling and rigging. Shape deformation offers a natural way to generate variations from existing meshes, but existing data-driven methods often rely on sparse user inputs, while parametric editing frameworks require manually designed control structures and category-specific configurations. Inspired by natural creatures, where a central spine governs global shape and cross-sectional ribs control local variation, we introduce Fishbone, a unified rib-spine representation for general shapes that supports controllable parametric mesh deformation, reduced-space dynamics, and animation. Given an input mesh, Fishbone computes a geodesic scalar field with an adaptive heat method, extracts iso-contours as cross-sectional ribs, constructs a smooth geometry-aware spine through rib centers, and associates surface vertices with nearby rib and spine structures using Gaussian-weighted skinning. The resulting representation enables real-time and predictable deformation: ribs control local profiles such as thickness, orientation, and cross-sectional variation, while the spine controls global bending, twisting, and stretching. The same structure also supports reduced-space simulation and keyframe animation. We further construct Fishbone-136K by augmenting Hunyuan3D with rib-spine structures, and demonstrate applications in controllable 3D generation, deformation-based data augmentation for robot learning, interactive mesh editing, and agentic generation. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and versatility of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [79] [CLIP-Guided SAM: Parameter-Efficient Semantic Conditioning for Promptable Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24807)
*Shayan Jalilian,Abdul Bais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIP-Guided SAM，通过轻量级多模态语义适配器将CLIP的文本、视觉和相似性特征注入SAM的图像编码器，实现内部语义条件化分割，支持手动和半自动两种模式，在低标注数据场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM等可提示基础模型生成高质量掩码但缺乏语义感知能力，需要外部提示指定类别。现有视觉-语言方法通过外部提示耦合（用视觉语言模型生成空间提示给SAM）来解决，但这种方式将语义信号与分割过程分离，效率较低。

Method: 提出内部语义条件化框架，通过轻量级多模态语义适配器将CLIP提取的文本、视觉和相似性特征注入SAM的图像编码器，直接调节SAM的内部特征表示，同时保留其原始的可提示交互接口。支持手动模式（文本+空间提示）和半自动文本模式（仅文本输入）。

Result: 在通用基准和专用下游任务上，与SAM+PEFT基线、视觉语言+SAM流水线、SAM 3以及强半监督分割方法相比，CLIP-Guided SAM在参数效率更高的条件下持续取得优越或竞争力相当的性能。

Conclusion: 训练与推理时使用的提示类型需保持一致（train-test prompt consistency）是重要的设计原则；该方法在低标注数据场景下有效，兼具参数效率与性能优势。

Abstract: Promptable foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) produce high-quality masks but remain semantically blind, relying on external prompts to specify categories. Existing vision-language approaches address this limitation by using external prompt coupling, where a vision-language model generates spatial prompts for SAM as a separate stage.
  We propose CLIP-Guided SAM, a parameter-efficient segmentation framework built on internal semantic conditioning. Instead of using semantic signals only to generate prompts, we inject CLIP-derived text, vision, and similarity features directly into SAM's image encoder through lightweight multi-modal semantic adapters. These adapters condition SAM's internal feature representations, allowing semantic information to influence mask prediction while preserving SAM's original promptable interface.
  Our framework is designed for low labeled-data settings and applies to both general-domain benchmarks and specialized downstream tasks. It supports two operating modes: Manual mode, for interactive segmentation with both text and spatial prompts, and Semi-Automatic text-only mode, for applications that require concept-specific segmentation using only textual input. We show that robustness depends on aligning training with the type of prompts used at inference, making train-test prompt consistency an important design principle.
  Through extensive experiments and ablations, we evaluate our method against SAM+PEFT baselines without semantic conditioning, vision-language + SAM pipelines, SAM 3, and strong semi-supervised segmentation methods that rely on large amounts of unlabeled data. Across these settings, CLIP-Guided SAM consistently achieves superior or competitive performance while remaining parameter-efficient in both training and deployment.

</details>


### [80] [Multiscale Real-Time Object Detection in the NMS-Free Era: A Comparative Performance Evaluation of YOLOv8 and YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24831)
*Chidera G. Oguine,Kanyifeechukwu J. Oguine,Obiozor M. Oguine,Ozioma C. Oguine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于NMS的YOLOv8和无NMS的YOLO26在Pascal VOC和VisDrone数据集上的性能，发现YOLO26在一般检测任务上表现更好且模型更小，但在密集小目标场景下两者差距缩小，且YOLOv8在GPU延迟上仍具竞争力，表明检测器选择需综合考虑数据集、目标尺度、模型容量和硬件约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMS作为实时目标检测的后处理步骤会引入延迟波动和部署复杂性，尽管已有YOLO26等无NMS设计试图减少这一依赖，但其与基于NMS的模型（如YOLOv8）在标准基准之外的全面性能对比尚不充分，因此需要系统评估两者在不同场景下的表现。

Method: 选取YOLOv8（基于NMS）和YOLO26（无NMS）两个模型家族，在Pascal VOC（通用目标检测）和VisDrone（密集航拍小目标检测）两个数据集上，跨越五个模型尺度，从检测精度、定位能力、模型大小、GFLOPs、CPU/GPU延迟等多个维度进行系统比较。

Result: 在Pascal VOC上，YOLO26在大多数尺度下取得了更强的检测性能和更低的模型复杂度；在VisDrone上，两者性能差距缩小，且均对密集小目标检测效果不佳。YOLOv8在GPU延迟上仍保持竞争力，表明无NMS设计并不天然具有部署优势。

Conclusion: 检测器的选择并非单一指标决定，需综合考虑数据集特性、目标尺度分布、模型容量和硬件平台约束，NMS-free设计在部分场景有优势但并非普适最优。

Abstract: Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) remains a key post-processing step in many real-time object detection pipelines, but it can introduce latency variation and deployment complexity in resource-constrained settings. Recent NMS-free designs such as YOLO26 aim to reduce this dependence through end-to-end detection, yet their performance relative to established NMS-based models such as YOLOv8 remains underexplored beyond standard benchmarks. This paper compares YOLOv8 and YOLO26 on Pascal VOC and VisDrone, representing general object detection and dense aerial small-object detection, respectively. Both model families are evaluated across five scales using accuracy, localization, model size, GFLOPs, and CPU/GPU latency. Results show that YOLO26 achieves stronger detection performance and lower model complexity on Pascal VOC across most scales, while the performance gap narrows on VisDrone, where both models struggle with dense small targets. YOLOv8 remains competitive in GPU latency, showing that NMS-free design does not guarantee universal deployment superiority. Overall, the study shows that detector selection depends on dataset characteristics, object scale, model capacity, and hardware constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Document Classification Pattern Recognition via Information Fusion: A Systematic Review of Multimodal and Multiview Representation Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23910)
*Marcin Michał Mirończuk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述和元分析，提出了信息融合用于文档分类的统一框架，量化了多模态融合（准确率提升+5.28%）和多视图融合（准确率+4.67%，F1+3.08%）的性能增益，并揭示了方法学严谨性不足的问题（仅11.8%-23.3%的研究使用了统计检验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息融合虽已广泛用于改进文档分类，但该领域缺乏统一框架、对有效性的定量综合以及面向实践者的清晰指导。

Method: 分析了139项主要研究，提出形式化框架，进行定性分析以识别关键趋势，并执行随机效应元分析（据我们所知是首个聚焦文档分类的元分析）以量化性能提升。

Result: 多模态融合使准确率显著提升（平均+5.28个百分点，p=0.0016），F1分数方向为正但统计不显著；多视图融合在准确率（+4.67%）、F1（+3.08%）和召回率上均有一致但适度的提升（均p<0.05）。仅11.8%（多模态）和23.3%（多视图）的研究使用了统计检验。

Conclusion: 成功的信息融合不取决于算法复杂性，而取决于融合方法与任务上下文的战略对齐以及对更严谨验证的承诺。

Abstract: Information fusion is used widely to improve document classification by the integration of multiple data sources (multimodal) or representations (multiview). However, the field lacks a unified framework, a quantitative synthesis of its effectiveness, and clear guidance for practitioners. This systematic review addresses these gaps by analysing 139 primary studies. It introduces a formal framework to structure the field, presents the results of a qualitative analysis to identify key trends, and performs a random-effects meta-analysis (to our knowledge, the first focused on document classification) to quantify performance gains. Our meta-analysis reveals that multimodal fusion improves accuracy (mean gain of +5.28 percentage points, $p=0.0016$) significantly -- the F1-score effect is directionally positive but statistically non-significant in our primary model. Multiview fusion provides consistent but modest gains for accuracy (+4.67\%), F1-score (+3.08\%), and recall (all $p<0.05$). Critically, our qualitative synthesis uncovers challenges in reproducibility in methodological rigour: only 11.8\% (multimodal) and 23.3\% (multiview) of the studies use statistical tests to validate their findings, which undermines the reliability of many of their results. This review's primary contributions are a unifying framework, the first quantitative evidence base, and data-driven guidelines. This review concludes that successful information fusion depends not on algorithmic complexity, but on the strategic alignment of the fusion method with the task context and a commitment to more rigorous validation.

</details>


### [82] [Raon-Speech Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23912)
*Beomsoo Kim,Changho Choi,Dohyun Kim,Dongki Lee,Ethan Ewer,Eunchong Kim,Gyeongman Kim,Haechan Kim,Hyeonghwan Kim,Inkyu Park,Jihun Yun,Jihwan Moon,Jiyun Kim,Joonghyun Bae,Junhyuck Kim,Minkyu Kim,Sehun Lee,Seungjun Chung,Sungwoo Cho,Dongmin Park,Dongwon Kim,Hara Kang,Jonghyun Lee,Keon Lee,Kangwook Lee,Jaewoong Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 Raon-Speech（9B参数语音语言模型）和 Raon-SpeechChat（全双工对话扩展），在42项英韩语音及文本基准测试中优于Qwen2.5-Omni等8个同类模型，开源全部检查点与训练推理代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练大语言模型转化为既能理解又能生成语音的语音语言模型，同时保持强大的文本能力，并进一步扩展到自然的全双工实时对话。

Method: Raon-Speech采用三阶段训练：(1)语音模块对齐，(2)端到端预训练+知识蒸馏，(3)多任务偏好优化后训练。Raon-SpeechChat采用三阶段：(1)因果编码器适配，(2)全双工预训练，(3)全双工微调（语音与角色控制）。使用1.38M小时英韩语音文本数据和119K小时时间对齐对话数据。

Result: 在42项英韩语音文本基准测试中，Raon-Speech在语音任务上全面超越Qwen2.5-Omni等8个同类模型，同时保持强文本问答能力。Raon-SpeechChat在全双工基准FDB v1.0上展现明显的轮换与打断敏感优势。

Conclusion: 提出了性能领先的语音语言模型及全双工对话模型，开源了完整资源，为语音理解和生成一体化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present Raon-Speech, a top-performing 9B-parameter speech language model (SpeechLM) for English and Korean speech understanding, answering, and generation, and Raon-SpeechChat, a high-performing full-duplex extension for natural real-time conversation. Raon-Speech successfully transforms a pre-trained LLM into a SpeechLM that both understands and generates speech while preserving strong text capabilities. It trains on 1.38M hours of highly curated English and Korean speech and text datasets with the following training stages: (1) speech modules alignment, (2) end-to-end SpeechLM pre-training with knowledge distillation, and (3) multi-task preference optimization-based post-training. Across 42 English and Korean speech and text benchmarks, Raon-Speech establishes the strongest overall profile on speech-centric tasks in our comparison against eight similarly sized recent audio foundation models, including Qwen2.5-Omni and Fun-Audio-Chat, while preserving strong text question answering performance. Building upon it, Raon-SpeechChat enables natural full-duplex conversation by continual training on 119K hours of time-aligned real and synthetic dialogue data. It proceeds through three complementary training stages: (1) causal encoder adaptation, (2) full-duplex pre-training, (3) full-duplex fine-tuning for voice and role-control. On multiple full-duplex benchmarks, Raon-SpeechChat shows its clearest strengths on the turn-taking and interruption-sensitive behaviors covered by FDB v1.0, and remains competitive across the broader full-duplex evaluation suite. We open-source all model checkpoints, the training and inference pipeline, and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-Persona Debate System for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23917)
*Jaeha Oh,Byungchan Kim,Ju Li,Yang Jeong Park,Jin-Sung Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多角色辩论系统(MPDS)，通过文献检索、长上下文LLM推理、语料驱动角色归纳和结构化多智能体辩论，实现自动化科学假设生成。在电池材料设计中验证，该方法比基线方法产生更机制明确、过程意识更强的方案，并在消融研究中取得最高综合假设质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学发现的瓶颈不是数据稀缺，而是无法将碎片化知识综合为可操作的假设，尤其在电池材料研究中电化学性能、界面行为与制造可行性需同时优化，现有方法难以有效整合多方面约束。

Method: MPDS包含四个核心模块：(1)文献检索构建最多500篇论文的快照；(2)长上下文LLM推理；(3)基于语料库的角色归纳；(4)结构化多智能体辩论，经过三轮引文感知辩论后由主持人综合，保留证据可追溯性。评估采用时间控制协议，排除对目标论文的直接访问。

Result: 在钠离子负极和全固态电池正极设计任务中，MPDS恢复了与实验验证解空间一致的设计逻辑，生成比基线更机制明确、过程感知的提议。引入综合假设质量评分，MPDS在消融研究中获得最高平均分，最大优势体现在跨视角整合能力。实验室后续验证表明其可作为工作流瓶颈诊断辅助工具。

Conclusion: 在文献快照上进行结构化辩论能够改善耦合工程约束下的假设形成，为文本密集型科学发现提供了可复用的工作流。

Abstract: Modern scientific discovery is bottlenecked not by data scarcity, but by the inability to synthesize fragmented knowledge into actionable hypotheses. This challenge is especially acute in battery materials research, where electrochemical performance, interfacial behavior, and manufacturing feasibility must be optimized simultaneously. Here, we present the Multi-Persona Debate System (MPDS), a literature-grounded framework for automated scientific hypothesis generation that combines literature retrieval, long-context large language model reasoning, corpus-driven persona induction, and structured multi-agent debate. MPDS constructs literature snapshots of up to 500 papers, grounds agents in role-specific evidence pools, and conducts a three-round citation-aware debate followed by moderator synthesis, enabling negotiation between personas while preserving evidence traceability. We evaluate MPDS using a temporally controlled protocol excluding direct access to target papers, including two held-out battery-materials case studies and a blinded comparison across 30 matched cases. In sodium-ion anode and all-solid-state battery cathode design tasks, MPDS recovered design logics aligned with experimentally validated solution spaces and generated more mechanistically explicit, process-aware proposals than simpler baselines. To assess the impact of personas and debate, we introduce Integrative Hypothesis Quality scoring. In ablation studies, MPDS achieved the highest mean score among five conditions, with its largest advantage in cross-perspective integration. A laboratory follow-up suggests utility as a diagnostic aid for identifying practical bottlenecks in workflows. These results indicate that structured debate over literature snapshots improves hypothesis formation under coupled engineering constraints and provides a reusable workflow for text-intensive scientific discovery.

</details>


### [84] [Improving the Completeness and Comparability of Segment Disclosures: A Large Language Model Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23924)
*Yue Liu,Zhiyuan Cheng,Longying Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型的框架，用于从10-K文件中直接提取分部披露信息（包括可报告分部与嵌套分部），并设计检索增强系统以支持跨企业、跨时间维度的可比性分析，实验证明该方法能准确提取信息并有效回答跨期问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化数据库在分部信息方面存在完整性和可比性不足的问题：部分公司-年度观测值缺失、嵌套分部披露未被捕获、纵向和跨企业可比性受限。

Method: 基于大语言模型构建提取框架，直接从10-K文件中提取分部披露信息，保留可报告和嵌套分部信息；设计检索增强系统，整合多个文件的信息以支持可比性。

Result: 该框架能准确提取分部层面信息，在两个代表性场景（企业内部分部变动纵向分析、不同报告结构企业间地理分部对齐）中有效回答需要跨期知识的查询。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法在提升分部披露信息的度量与解释方面具有潜力，可增强实证研究的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Segment-level disclosures are a central component of financial reporting, providing insight into firms' internal organization and the allocation of economic activities across operating units. However, segment information is often presented in both qualitative and quantitative forms, dispersed across tables and narrative sections of Form 10-K filings. Empirical research relying on structured databases faces both completeness and comparability challenges, as some firm-year observations may be missing, nested segment disclosures are not captured, and support for longitudinal and cross-firm comparability is limited. This study develops a large language model-based framework to extract segment disclosures directly from Form 10-K filings and to preserve both reportable and nested segment information. We further design a retrieval augmented system that incorporates information across multiple filings to support comparability. We use two representative settings to demonstrate its application: longitudinal analysis within a firm to interpret segment changes over time, and cross firm alignment of geographic segments across firms with different reporting structures. The results indicate that the artifact accurately extracts segment-level information and effectively addresses questions that require cross-period knowledge, demonstrating the potential of LLM-based approaches to enhance the measurement and interpretation of segment disclosures.

</details>


### [85] [EchoDistill:Alignment Noisy-to-Clean Self-Distillation for Robust Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23954)
*Liang Lin,Chunxi Luo,Kaiwen Luo,Jie Zhang,Jin Wang,Yuanhe Zhang,Cai Yuchen,Qiankun Li,Gongli Xi,Zhenhong Zhou,Kun Wang,Junhao Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出echodistill，一种基于对齐的噪声到清洁自蒸馏框架，通过冻结的清洁音频教师模型为噪声环境下推理的学生模型提供语义参考，利用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对齐学生候选响应与教师语义，显著提升音频大语言模型在复杂噪声下的语义可靠性和任务性能，且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型（ALLMs）在真实噪声环境下极易产生语义漂移和幻觉，现有鲁棒性方法（波形级声学增强、答案级监督、内部噪声表示抑制）不足以解决这一问题。

Method: 提出echodistill对齐式噪声到清洁自蒸馏框架：使用冻结的清洁音频教师模型提供语义参考，噪声环境下学生模型采样候选响应暴露测试时行为，通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）优化轨迹，以教师模型的token级一致性作为奖励，结合音频感知奖励塑造，对齐学生响应与清洁语义证据。

Result: 在强噪声下，相比最强基线，echodistill在GSR指标上平均提升4.18%；在Qwen-Omni上，相比纯GRPO变体，Acc提升3.02%，Noisy提升3.89%，GSR提升4.53%。

Conclusion: Echodistill有效提升音频LLM在复杂噪声下的语义可靠性和任务性能，且不引入额外推理成本，是一种轻量且高效的噪声鲁棒性增强方法。

Abstract: Audio Large Language Models (ALLMs) are highly vulnerable to real-world noise, which often induces severe semantic drift and hallucinations. Existing robustness methods primarily rely on waveform-level acoustic enhancement, answer-level supervision, or the internal suppression of noise representations. To address these issues, we propose echodistill, an alignment-based noisy-to-clean self-distillation framework. Echodistill leverages a frozen clean-audio teacher to provide semantic references for an inference-time noisy-audio student. Specifically, the student samples candidate responses under noisy conditions to expose its test-time behavior. These trajectories are then optimized via group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), where the token-level consistency with the teacher acts as a reward bonus. By aligning the noisy student's candidate responses with clean semantic evidence, and applying audio-aware reward shaping, our method encourages reasoning trajectories that are both correct and genuinely acoustically grounded. Echodistill significantly improves the semantic reliability and task performance of Audio LLMs under complex noise, without introducing any additional inference costs. Extensive experiments show that: (I) Compared with the strongest baseline, echodistill achieves average improvements of 4.18\%$\uparrow$ in GSR under strong noise. (II) Ablation results on Qwen-Omni further show that echodistill improves over the GRPO-only variant by 3.02\%$\uparrow$ in Acc, 3.89\%$\uparrow$ in Noisy, and 4.53\%$\uparrow$ in GSR on average. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/echodistill-10DE.

</details>


### [86] [TriVAL: A Tri-Validation Framework for Faithful Automatic Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23966)
*Ziyang Fang,JinXi Wang,Jinghui Zhong,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TriVAL三阶段验证框架（语义规范→数学公式→代码生成）和NL4COP基准测试集，在自动优化建模中通过逐阶段校验修正错误，显著提升复杂组合优化问题的建模准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化建模方法缺乏显式验证机制，早期阶段引入的错误会沿流水线累积传递，导致最终建模精度下降。

Method: 提出TriVAL三阶段验证框架：语义规范、数学公式、代码生成，每个阶段采用"构建-验证-修正"循环，按阶段特定标准评估并修正结果；同时构建NL4COP基准测试集（50种问题类型，150个实例）。

Result: 在NL4COP及现有基准测试上，TriVAL一致优于最先进方法，在最具挑战性的问题上提升最大。

Conclusion: 显式的分阶段验证机制能有效阻断错误跨阶段传递，提升自动优化建模的保真度和准确性，尤其适用于复杂组合优化问题。

Abstract: Optimization modeling serves as the pivotal bridge between natural-language problem descriptions and optimization solvers, and remains a cornerstone for bringing operations research (OR) into real-world decision making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in automatic optimization modeling. However, existing methods still lack explicit validation during the modeling process, allowing errors introduced in earlier stages to carry through the pipeline and ultimately reduce final modeling accuracy. To address this challenge, we introduce TriVAL, a tri-validation framework that performs explicit validation at three stages of automatic optimization modeling: semantic specification, mathematical formulation, and code generation. At each stage, TriVAL follows a construct-validate-revise loop that assesses the current result against stage-specific criteria and revises it when needed. This design helps identify and correct errors before they accumulate across stages, helping preserve faithfulness throughout the modeling process. To evaluate automatic optimization modeling on more challenging combinatorial problems, we further introduce NL4COP, a benchmark of 150 instances across 50 diverse problem types with more complex decision logic, more tightly coupled constraints, and more demanding modeling requirements than existing benchmarks. Experiments on NL4COP and established benchmarks show that TriVAL consistently outperforms state-ofthe-art methods, with the largest gains on the most challenging problems.

</details>


### [87] [SLAP: Stratified Loss-based Pruning for On-Policy Data-Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23969)
*Run Zou,Jianhang Ding,Yifan Ding,Wen Wu,Hao Chen,Renshu Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLAP是一种面向大语言模型指令微调的批感知数据选择框架，通过分布感知分层采样和相对距离优化实现批次级数据可学习性评估，在LLaMA、ChatGLM等模型上以20-40%更少训练数据达到或超越全量训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调虽能优化LLM能力，但依赖大规模数据集和长时间训练。现有方法缺乏对批次整体可学习性的评估，导致数据利用效率低下。SLAP旨在通过高效数据选择实现低成本、高性能的微调。

Method: 提出批感知数据选择框架SLAP：(1) 分布感知分层采样以覆盖完整数据分布；(2) 相对距离优化以最大化批次内多样性；(3) 基于Hessian近似梯度信息的动态批次选择，评估整个批次组合的可学习性而非单个样本。

Result: 在LLaMA、ChatGLM等多模型架构及多轮对话、多语言翻译、问答等下游任务上，SLAP显著优于现有SOTA方法，仅用全量训练数据的60-80%即可达到或超越全量训练性能，大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: SLAP是一种高效的指令微调数据选择方法，通过批次级可学习性评估和多样性优化，在减少训练数据的同时保持甚至提升模型能力，为LLM高效微调提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has optimized the specialized capabilities of large language models (LLMs), but it often requires extensive datasets and prolonged training times. The challenge lies in developing specific capabilities by identifying useful data and efficiently fine-tuning. High-quality and diverse pruned data can help models achieve lossless performance at a lower cost. In this paper, we propose \textbf{SLAP}, a novel batch-aware data selection framework that evaluates the learnability of entire batch compositions rather than individual. SLAP ensures comprehensive data distribution coverage through distribution-aware stratified sampling while maximizing intra-batch diversity through relative distance optimization. By leveraging Hessian-approximated gradient information for dynamic batch selection, SLAP significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods across multiple model architectures (LLaMA, ChatGLM) and diverse downstream tasks including multi-turn dialogue, multilingual translation, and question answering. Most notably, SLAP achieves superior performance with 20-40\% less training data compared to full dataset training, substantially reducing computational costs while maintaining or improving model capabilities. These results establish SLAP as a powerful approach for efficient and effective instruction tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [88] [Faithful or Fabricated? A Causal Framework for Rationalization Bias in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23970)
*Riya Tapwal,Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM作为自动评判者时是否具有**线索不变性**（即当非证据性线索被扰动时，排名和解释是否保持稳定）。作者引入一套线索干预方法和线索感知指标，发现LLM评判者对标签和安慰剂扰动存在显著的**线索锚定合理化**现象，并提出**PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE**方法有效提升线索不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM评判者的结果偏差（如位置、冗长度、风格偏好），但对评判解释的探索不足。本文旨在探究LLM评判者在非证据性线索被扰动时，其排名和解释是否保持稳定（即线索不变性）。

Method: 1) 设计一套**线索干预**方法：Blind、Truth、Flip、Placebo、Reveal-After；2) 提出**线索感知指标**，包括结果锚定和推理锚定（标签对齐修辞、解释漂移、一致性检查、刻板印象入侵检查）；3) 使用**冗长度和置信度线索**设计锚定攻击；4) 比较两种缓解策略：结构化思维链提示和**PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE**（证据锁定→评分→排序）；5) 构建包含1000篇来自传统抽取式模型和LLM摘要的数据集。

Result: 在标签和安慰剂扰动下，LLM评判者表现出显著的线索锚定合理化现象；而PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE方法相比基线显著提升了线索不变性。

Conclusion: LLM评判者对非证据性线索敏感且易产生锚定偏差，但通过结构化推理流程（先证据后偏好）可以有效缓解这一问题，提升评判的稳健性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automatic judges for summarization and dialogue evaluation. Prior work has documented biases such as position, verbosity, and style preferences, but largely focuses on outcomes, leaving judge explanations underexplored. We instead ask whether LLM judges are cue-invariant, i.e., whether their rankings and explanations remain stable when non-evidential cues are perturbed while holding the underlying texts fixed. We introduce a suite of cue interventions (Blind, Truth, Flip, Placebo, Reveal-After) and tie-aware metrics that quantify outcome anchoring and rationale anchoring, including label-aligned rhetoric and explanation drift, alongside consistency and stereotype-intrusion checks. We design anchoring attacks using verbosity and confidence cues, and compare two mitigations: structured chain-of-thought prompting and PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE (evidence lock, score, rank). Using a new dataset of 1,000 summaries from traditional extractive models and LLMs, we find substantial cue-anchored rationalization under label and placebo perturbations, while PROOF-BEFORE-PREFERENCE markedly improves cue invariance over baselines.

</details>


### [89] [AERIC: Anticipatory Hidden-State Monitoring for Implicit Harmful Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23974)
*Jihyung Park,Saleh Afroogh,Junfeng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AERIC方法，通过轻量级线性监测器（仅387个可训练参数）在解码过程中读取隐藏状态，实现对隐式有害对话的预判监测。相比Qwen3GuardStream-4B，AERIC在多项基准上提升AUROC，同时延迟仅增加2.34%（对比79.40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型安全监测存在两个挑战：一是需要在生成有害内容前尽早检测风险，二是危害可能是隐式的而非显式有毒文本。现有响应级防护和流式防护虽各有优势，但都未解决能否通过轻量级监测器从生成器内部轨迹预判隐式有害漂移的问题。

Method: 提出AERIC方法，采用面向迁移的隐式有害对话隐藏状态方法，结合短视界危害预测（short-horizon hazard forecasting）、支持敏感抑制（support-sensitive suppression）和提示条件残差评分（prompt-conditioned residual scoring），在相同前向传播（same-pass）下使用指数移动平均决策规则。默认线性监测器仅含387个可训练头部参数。

Result: 在平衡基准上，AERIC相比Qwen3GuardStream-4B将DiaSafety的AUROC从0.6830提升至0.7143，Harmful Advice从0.8219提升至0.8582。在提示级触发基准上，通过源端安全预算规则校准阈值，触发率@64在HarmBench DirectRequest上达0.6438/0.4656（Qwen/Gemma），SocialHarmBench上达0.6849/0.7363，平均扣留23.53–41.86个答案token。延迟仅增加2.34%，远低于对比方法的79.40%。

Conclusion: AERIC方法证明，通过轻量级线性监测器在解码过程中读取隐藏状态即可有效预判隐式有害内容，兼顾检测性能与极低延迟开销，为同前向传播安全监测提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Current language models create two safety challenges: risk must be detected early enough to avoid exposing harmful continuation, and the harmfulness itself may be implicit rather than signaled by overtly toxic text. Existing response-level guards are strong at judging completed text, and native streaming guards move closer to token time, but both settings leave open whether a lightweight monitor can anticipate implicit harmful drift from the generator's own internal trajectory. We study anticipatory same-pass monitoring, where a safety monitor may read hidden states produced during ordinary decoding but may not invoke an additional forward pass through the base model. We introduce AERIC, a transfer-oriented hidden-state approach for implicit harmful dialogue that combines short-horizon hazard forecasting, support-sensitive suppression, and prompt-conditioned residual scoring under a same-pass exponential moving average decision rule. The default linear monitor contains only 387 trainable head parameters. Against Qwen3GuardStream-4B on balanced benchmarks, AERIC improves AUROC from 0.6830 to 0.7143 on DiaSafety and from 0.8219 to 0.8582 on Harmful Advice. For promptlevel trigger benchmarks, we calibrate the AERIC threshold by a source-side safe-budget rule that maximizes trigger coverage while constraining the safe-trigger rate to at most 10%. Under that rule, trigger@64 reaches 0.6438 and 0.4656 on HarmBench DirectRequest and 0.6849 and 0.7363 on SocialHarmBench for Qwen and Gemma, respectively, withholding between 23.53 and 41.86 answer tokens on average. Same-pass deployment is also efficient: on a 63-prompt harmfulprompt fixed-generation benchmark aggregated over HarmBench DirectRequest and SocialHarmBench under Qwen3-8B, the monitor increases mean latency by only 2.34%, whereas Qwen3Guard-Stream-4B increases it by 79.40%.

</details>


### [90] [Direct Preference Optimization for English-Mandarin Code-Switching Speech Recognition in Audio LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23975)
*Trung Nguyen Quang,Cheng Yi Lewis Won,Minh Duc Pham,Yingxu He,Shuo Sun,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现音频大语言模型在代码转换语音转录中存在三种系统故障模式（语言省略、翻译代替转录、幻觉），通过构建偏好对并使用直接偏好优化（DPO）对三个模型进行对齐训练，使模型学会保留语言组成而非翻译，词错误率降低最高达89.6%（分布内）和20.0%（分布外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型虽然具有多语言能力，但在处理代码转换语音（中英混合）转录时存在系统性失败，需要有效方法纠正这些行为。

Method: 识别三种故障模式（语言省略、翻译代替转录、幻觉）；构建DPO偏好对（保留混合语言内容为选择响应，模仿故障模式为拒绝响应）；在100K偏好对（570小时）上对三个音频LLM进行DPO训练。

Result: 模型学会保留语言组成而非翻译；分布内MER降低最高89.6%，分布外降低最高20.0%；三个模型均表现出一致的行为转变。

Conclusion: DPO能够有效地从多语言音频LLM中激发正确的代码转换转录行为，是一种有效的行为对齐方法。

Abstract: Audio large language models (Audio LLMs) exhibit systematic failures in transcribing code-switching speech despite strong multilingual capabilities. Focusing on English-Mandarin, we identify three failure modes: language omission, translation-instead-of-transcription, and hallucination. We apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to align models, constructing preference pairs in which chosen responses preserve mixed-language content while rejected responses mimic failure patterns. Training three Audio LLMs on 100K pairs (570 hours), we observe consistent behavioral shifts: models learn to preserve language composition rather than translating when prompted for transcription. This alignment yields MER reductions up to 89.6% (in-distribution) and 20.0% (out-of-distribution). Our findings suggest DPO can effectively elicit correct code-switching transcription behavior from multilingual Audio LLMs.

</details>


### [91] [A Multi-Probe Audit of Clinical-Interview Depression Detection Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23977)
*Takehiro Ishikawa,Jon Duke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过四项互补探针审计临床访谈抑郁检测中的基准评估，涵盖五个数据集（DAIC/E-DAIC, CMDC, ANDROIDS, MODMA, PDCH）。主要发现包括：1）在严格留一法交叉验证下，轻量级文本+LLM评分模型达到macro-F1=0.723（已知最高）；2）E-DAIC官方划分下，开发集交叉验证与官方测试排名仅中等程度对齐（top-3重叠为零）；3）CMDC和ANDROIDS基线在域内表现接近天花板，但零样本跨语料迁移显著较弱；4）压力测试显示文本模型对症状密集片段敏感度远高于音频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床访谈抑郁检测基准评估存在方法论缺陷，如未采用严格的受试者不重复交叉验证、官方划分的排名可靠性未知、跨数据集泛化能力未充分检验等。本文旨在系统审计这些基准评估协议，揭示其潜在问题。

Method: 四项互补探针：1）严格留一法交叉验证评估E-DAIC；2）通过96种模型配置扫描检验官方划分排名的稳健性；3）外部验证CMDC和ANDROIDS基线并测试零样本迁移；4）基于SRDS症状密度注释对E-DAIC进行压力测试。

Result: 1）轻量级混合模型在严格留一法下达到macro-F1=0.723；2）开发集交叉验证与官方测试排名仅中等程度相关，最优交叉验证配置在官方测试中仅排第20；3）域内性能接近天花板但跨语料零样本迁移大幅下降；4）文本模型在症状密集片段上性能显著提升而音频模型几乎不变。

Conclusion: 当前抑郁检测基准评估存在显著的方法论问题，包括排名不稳定、跨域泛化不足以及模态间行为差异，未来需要更严格的评估协议和跨数据集验证标准。

Abstract: This paper audits benchmark evaluation in clinical-interview depression detection through four complementary probes across DAIC/E-DAIC, CMDC, ANDROIDS, MODMA, and PDCH. First, we re-evaluate E-DAIC under strict subject-disjoint leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. A lightweight hybrid text-plus-LLM-score model reaches macro-F1 = 0.723 - the highest reported under this protocol, to our knowledge - providing a conservative out-of-fold reference point that does not depend on the privileged official holdout. Second, we test whether the E-DAIC official split supports fine-grained leaderboard rankings by sweeping 96 model configurations across modality bundles, pooling strategies, and learners. Development-side cross-validation and official-test rankings align only moderately: the best cross-validation configuration ranks twentieth on the official test, the official-test winner ranks forty-first by cross-validation, top-3 overlap is zero, and the apparent winner is rank-1 in only 32.3% of subject bootstraps. Third, we externally validate strong public CMDC and ANDROIDS baselines that achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance. Zero-shot transfer to external corpora is substantially weaker. Finally, we stress-test E-DAIC text and audio models using paired symptom-dense versus symptom-light interview slices defined by an SRDS-based annotator. Text scores rise sharply on symptom-dense slices, whereas audio scores remain nearly flat; the text-minus-audio gap is positive across all five seeds.

</details>


### [92] [Toxicity in Twitch Chats: An LLM-Based Analysis Across Gaming Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24000)
*Ronja Fuchs,Florian Rupp,Timo Bertram,Kai Eckert,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了Twitch平台上7个游戏类型的4,452个直播间约2000万条聊天消息，使用大语言模型零样本分类进行毒性检测（F1=94.5%），发现2.4%的消息有毒，MOBA游戏毒性最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%），且毒性分布在不同游戏间存在显著差异，表明存在游戏特定的社区规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于游戏内毒性，对直播平台游戏社区中毒性行为的差异了解不足，因此需要系统性地量化不同游戏类型和具体游戏在直播平台上的毒性分布。

Method: 收集Twitch平台7个游戏类型的4,452个直播间约2000万条聊天消息；使用预训练大语言模型（LLM）基于Twitch毒性分类体系进行零样本分类，将消息分为四类八子类（骚扰、歧视、性内容、脏话）；采用TextDetox数据集评估，F1达到94.5%。

Result: 2.4%的消息被分类为有毒；MOBA游戏毒性率最高（3.2%），体育游戏最低（2%）；即使在同一游戏类型内，不同游戏的毒性分布也存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 游戏类型和具体游戏都会影响直播聊天中的毒性行为，存在游戏特定的社区规范；研究为直播平台和游戏社区的针对性内容审核策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Toxicity in online gaming communities remains a persistent challenge, manifesting across genres, platforms, and player interactions. While much research is focused on in-game toxicity, less is known about how toxic behavior varies between gaming communities on streaming platforms. To address this shortcoming, we analyze approximately 20 million chat messages from 4,452 streams, spanning seven game genres on Twitch. We categorize messages according to Twitch's toxicity taxonomy with a pre-trained Large Language Model using zero-shot classification. The taxonomy comprises four categories and eight subclasses, including harassment, discrimination, sexual content, and profanity. Our approach achieves an F1 score of 94.5% on the TextDetox dataset and demonstrates human-model agreement comparable to inter-human agreement. Our analysis reveals that 2.4% of all messages are classified as toxic, with notable differences across genres: streams of MOBA games exhibit the highest relative rate of toxicity (3.2%), and sports games show the lowest rate (2%). Furthermore, results indicate that individual games differ significantly in their toxicity distributions, even within genres, suggesting the existence of game-specific community norms and mechanics that shape toxic behavior beyond genre-level effects. These findings offer empirical insights into genre- and game-specific toxicity patterns on Twitch and can inform more targeted moderation strategies for gaming communities.

</details>


### [93] [CUNY at CLPsych 2026: A Pipeline Approach to Classification and Summarization of Mental Health Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24164)
*Amirmohammad Ziaei Bideh,Shameed Charlomar Job,Ava Yahyapour,Alla Rozovskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了作者在CLPsych 2026共享任务中的提交系统，通过集成三个开源大语言模型的上下文学习（多数投票）来推断社交媒体帖子的主导自我状态（任务1.1和1.2）；利用任务1.1的预测特征训练监督分类器来预测时间线中的变化时刻（任务2）；通过增强上游系统预测的上下文示例标签来总结情绪动态模式及其随时间进展（任务3.1）。最终排名：任务1.1第一，任务1.2第四，任务2第四，任务3.1第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过社交媒体时间线动态来捕捉和表征心理健康变化，利用大语言模型和机器学习方法解决多任务心理健康分析问题。

Method: 对任务1.1和1.2，采用三个开源大语言模型的上下文学习集成，通过多数投票决定最终预测；对任务2，基于任务1.1的预测特征训练监督分类器来预测变化时刻；对任务3.1，使用上游系统（任务1.1、1.2和2）预测的标签来增强上下文示例，相比零样本和未增强的上下文学习基线获得性能提升。

Result: 排名：任务1.1第一，任务1.2第四，任务2第四，任务3.1第三。

Conclusion: 多阶段流水线方法在心理健康变化检测任务上表现良好，特别是上下文学习集成和预测标签增强策略带来了显著性能提升。

Abstract: We describe our submission to the CLPsych~2026 Shared Task on capturing and characterizing mental health changes through social media timeline dynamics. To infer the dominant self-states in posts (Tasks 1.1 and 1.2), we ensemble in-context learning of three open-weight large language models using majority voting. For predicting moments of change in a timeline (Task~2), we train supervised classifiers on features derived from Task~1.1 predictions. To summarize the patterns of mood dynamics and their progression over time within a timeline (Task 3.1), we augment in-context example labels predicted by upstream systems (Tasks 1.1, 1.2, and 2), yielding performance gains over zero-shot and unaugmented in-context learning baselines. Our submission ranked first on Task~1.1, fourth on Task~1.2, fourth on Task~2, and third on Task~3.1.\footnote{The source code for the experiments is available at https://github.com/amirzia/clpsych26-cuny

</details>


### [94] [Extracting Training Data from Diffusion Language Models via Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24173)
*Yihan Wang,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散语言模型（DLMs）的记忆化风险远高于传统认知——通过引入"填充提取"协议（用任意二进制掩码参数化数据提取），发现边缘条件掩码比前缀条件掩码最多可提取3倍的逐字序列，且DLMs对已脱敏训练数据中个人身份信息的提取召回率甚至高于同等规模的自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅通过前缀条件提取来评估大语言模型的记忆化，这忽略了扩散语言模型在任意位置去噪掩码标记的双向归纳偏置能力，导致严重低估训练数据提取风险。

Method: 提出"填充提取"协议，以任意二进制掩码参数化数据提取过程，统一了前缀探测并纳入了DLM的双向特性。在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型上，使用五种提取模式、三种训练流水线和三个语料库进行实验。

Result: 掩码几何形状主导可提取性：边缘条件掩码比前缀条件掩码最多多提取3倍的逐字序列；双向访问打开了自回归模型不可能存在的通道；在面对已脱敏训练数据时，DLM对已删除电子邮件地址的提取召回率高于同等规模的自回归模型。

Conclusion: 解码的可调参数显著影响提取性能，而后续的有监督微调阶段并不能消除先前存在的记忆化风险。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models has been studied almost exclusively through prefix-conditioned extraction, a natural choice for autoregressive models. However, diffusion language models (DLMs) can denoise masked tokens at arbitrary positions. Thus, prefix-only probing reveals only one facet of memorization in DLMs and significantly underestimates the risk of training-data extraction. In order to realistically model extractability of training data in DLMs, we introduce \emph{infilling extraction}, a data-extraction protocol parameterized by an arbitrary binary mask that subsumes prefix-only probing and accounts for the bidirectional inductive bias of DLMs. Instantiating it on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B across five extraction modes, three training pipelines, and three corpora covering verbatim and partial leakage, we find that mask geometry governs extractability: edge-conditioned masks \emph{extract up to three times more} verbatim sequences than prefix-conditioned ones, and bidirectional access opens channels inaccessible in autoregressive models. In particular, we show that a realistic adversary with access to training data where personally identifiable information has been redacted, can even achieve higher recall on extracting redacted email addresses from DLMs than from scale-matched autoregressive models. Tunable parameters for decoding measurably affect extraction performance, while a follow-up supervised finetuning stage does not eliminate the prior memorization.

</details>


### [95] [Teaching Through Analogies: A Modular Pipeline for Educational Analogy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24211)
*Mariam Barakat,Ekaterina Kochmar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化管道用于教育类比生成，将任务分解为源查找、子概念生成、解释生成和评估四个阶段。实验评估了12个LLM和7个嵌入模型，发现子概念能显著提升解释质量和封闭检索精度，但开放源生成中收益有限；同时提出了LLM作为评判者的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM取得进展，但在生成与人类质量相当的类比方面仍有困难。现有研究孤立地看待类比生成的各阶段，无法捕捉跨阶段交互作用，缺乏系统性的分析框架。

Method: 提出一个基于结构映射理论的四阶段模块化管道：源查找、子概念生成、解释生成和评估。在SCAR和ParallelPARC两个数据集上评估12个LLM（6个模型家族）和7个嵌入模型，并引入LLM-as-a-judge评估方法，与7名人工标注者的评分进行对比验证。

Result: 子概念显著提升了解释质量和封闭设置下的检索精度，但在开放源生成中收益有限。Claude Sonnet 4.6在排名一致性上与人类评判更可靠，但在细粒度绝对评分上表现不佳。揭示了跨阶段交互作用，强调子概念是类比质量生成的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 子概念基础是提升类比生成质量的关键；模块化管道设计有助于系统分析各阶段交互；LLM作为评判者在排名任务中更可靠；开放源生成仍具挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Analogies help learners understand unfamiliar concepts by relating them to known concepts. Despite recent advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to struggle to generate analogies of comparable quality to those produced by humans. We present a modular pipeline for educational analogy generation, decomposing the task into four stages: source finding, sub-concept generation, explanation generation, and evaluation. Grounded in Structure Mapping Theory, the pipeline enables systematic, stage-by-stage analysis of how model choice and input configuration affect analogy quality. We evaluate 12 state-of-the-art LLMs across six model families on two datasets with structured sub-concept annotations (SCAR and ParallelPARC), alongside seven embedding models for closed-setting retrieval. Our results show that sub-concepts substantially improve explanation quality and closed setting retrieval precision but provide limited benefit in open-ended source generation. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-judge evaluation methodology and validate its scoring against human annotations from seven annotators, finding that Claude Sonnet 4.6 aligns more reliably with human rankings than with fine-grained absolute scores. Taken together, our findings reveal cross-stage interactions that isolated studies cannot capture, and highlight sub-concept grounding as a key driver of analogy quality generation.

</details>


### [96] [QUEST: Training Frontier Deep Research Agents with Fully Synthetic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24218)
*Jian Xie,Tianhe Lin,Zilu Wang,Yuting Ning,Yuekun Yao,Tianci Xue,Zhehao Zhang,Zhongyang Li,Kai Zhang,Yufan Wu,Shijie Chen,Boyu Gou,Mingzhe Han,Yifei Wang,Vint Lee,Xinpeng Wei,Xiangjun Wang,Yu Su,Huan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布QUEST，一个2B-35B参数规模的开源深度研究智能体模型家族，通过结合中间训练、监督微调和强化学习的训练方案，以及基于统一评分树的数据合成流水线，仅用8K合成任务即可在多项深度研究基准上接近甚至超越前沿闭源智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体多为闭源系统，而开源智能体在不同任务类型上泛化能力差，缺乏训练通用深度研究智能体的有效方法论。

Method: 提出结合mid-training、监督微调(Supervised Fine-Tuning)和强化学习(Reinforcement Learning)的训练方案；核心是基于统一评分树(Unified Rubric Trees)的数据合成流水线，无需人工标注即可生成带有可验证奖励的训练数据；同时设计了内置的上下文管理机制以支持长程推理和知识综合。

Result: 仅使用8K合成任务训练，QUEST在8个深度研究基准上接近甚至超越前沿闭源智能体，并在近期开源智能体中取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练方案和数据合成方法能有效训练出通用的深度研究智能体，且完全开源（模型、数据、训练脚本）以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Deep research agents extend the role of search engines from retrieving keyword-matched pages to synthesizing knowledge, fundamentally changing how humans interact with information. However, frontier systems remain proprietary, while existing open agents often generalize poorly across different task types, leaving unclear how to train a broadly capable deep research agent. We release QUEST, a family of open models (ranging from 2B to 35B) that serve as general-purpose deep research agents designed to handle a wide range of long-horizon search tasks, with strong capabilities in fact seeking, citation grounding, and report synthesis. To build QUEST, we propose an effective training recipe combining mid-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Central to this recipe is a curated data synthesis pipeline based on unified rubric trees, which applies to different task types and enables synthesizing training data with verifiable rewards without human annotation. In addition, QUEST incorporates a built-in context management mechanism that enables effective long-horizon reasoning and knowledge synthesis. Using only 8K synthesized tasks, QUEST approaches or even surpasses frontier closed-source agents across eight deep research benchmarks spanning diverse task types, and achieves the best overall performance among recent open-weight agents. We released everything: models, data, and training scripts.

</details>


### [97] [Improving Labeling Consistency with Detailed Constitutional Definitions and AI-Driven Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24247)
*Konstantin Berlin,Adam Swanda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动的工作流，通过AI帮助编写详细的类别"宪法"定义，再由前沿LLM据此生成黄金标签。实验在三种内容审核类别上，将跨模型不一致性降低高达57倍，并引入双轴评分框架分别评估意图和内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化标注流程需要准确一致的黄金标签，但简单的类别定义不够详细，而过于详细的定义超出人类标注者工作记忆容量，导致标注者依赖直觉、标签偏离书面规则，准确性和一致性下降。

Method: 提出AI驱动工作流：(1) AI辅助为每个类别编写详细的"宪法"定义，覆盖足够多的边界案例；(2) 前沿LLM基于该宪法对每个输入进行推理，生成黄金标签。同时引入双轴评分公式，对完整对话独立评分意图和内容。

Result: 在骚扰、仇恨言论、非暴力犯罪三个内容审核类别上，该方法相比段落式定义将跨模型不一致性降低高达57倍；跨模型分歧可用于诊断规范缺陷，人类负责高层次类别定义决策而非单个标注判断。

Conclusion: AI驱动的宪法式标注工作流能显著提升跨模型一致性和标签质量，让人类从具体标注决策中解脱，专注于类别定义的高层决策。双轴评分框架为安全评估提供了更细粒度的控制能力。

Abstract: Many automated labeling pipelines classify inputs into categories defined by a written specification, content moderation being a prominent use case. Simple category definitions are not detailed enough for labelers to produce the accurate, consistent golden labels these pipelines require. One solution is to write a prescriptive definition that settles enough real boundary cases that labelers cannot disagree with the written interpretation. In practice, definitions at that level of detail exceed what a human annotator can hold in working memory, so annotators fall back on intuition and the labels drift from the written rules, regressing on accuracy and consistency.
  We propose and demonstrate the efficacy of an AI-driven workflow in which AI helps write a per-category constitution that defines the label in enough detail to cover edge cases, and a frontier LLM interprets it on each input to produce the golden label more consistently and accurately than humans reading the same document. We evaluate on three content moderation categories (harassment, hate speech, non-violent crime) and show that the approach reduces cross-model inconsistency by up to 57x compared to paragraph definitions, with cross-model disagreement diagnosing specification gaps and the human responsible for high-level decisions about what each category should mean rather than individual labeling calls. For the safety evaluation, we introduce a dual-axis formulation scoring intent and content independently over the full conversation, so downstream consumers can act on either axis or both.

</details>


### [98] [An Interactive Paradigm for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24266)
*Lin Ai,Victor S. Bursztyn,Xiang Chen,Julia Hirschberg,Saayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteER是一个可交互控制的深度研究框架，通过在决策点引入成本-收益机制来决定是否暂停征求用户输入，结合多样性感知规划与效用信号（对齐、新颖性、覆盖率），并维护动态演进的用户画像模型。在多个基准上对齐度提升最高达22.80%，人类读者在85%+的对齐判断中偏好SteER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统依赖刚性工作流，采用一次性范围设定和长时间自主运行，用户意图在过程中发生变化时缺乏纠偏机制，导致最终结果与用户真实需求脱节。

Method: SteER在每个决策点使用成本-收益公式判断是暂停等待用户输入还是继续自主运行；采用多样性感知规划结合对齐、新颖性和覆盖率等效用信号；维护一个在会话过程中动态演进的实时用户画像模型。

Result: SteER在多个基准上对齐度比最先进的开源和专有基线提升最高22.80%，在广度、平衡性等质量指标上领先，人类读者在85%+的对齐判断中更偏好SteER。

Conclusion: 这是首个在深度研究中引入可交互、可解释控制范式的工作，为长文本任务中可控、用户对齐的智能体开辟了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled deep research systems that synthesize comprehensive, report-style answers to open-ended queries by combining retrieval, reasoning, and generation. Yet most frameworks rely on rigid workflows with one-shot scoping and long autonomous runs, offering little room for course correction if user intent shifts mid-process. We present SteER, a framework for Steerable deEp Research that introduces interpretable, mid-process control into long-horizon research workflows. At each decision point, SteER uses a cost-benefit formulation to determine whether to pause for user input or to proceed autonomously. It combines diversity-aware planning with utility signals that reward alignment, novelty, and coverage, and maintains a live persona model that evolves throughout the session. SteER outperforms state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary baselines by up to 22.80\% on alignment, leads on quality metrics such as breadth and balance, and is preferred by human readers in 85\%+ of pairwise alignment judgments. We also introduce a persona-query benchmark and data-generation pipeline. To our knowledge, this is the first work to advance deep research with an interactive, interpretable control paradigm, paving the way for controllable, user-aligned agents in long-form tasks.

</details>


### [99] [DRInQ: Evaluating Conversational Implicature with Controlled Context Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24267)
*Hirona Jacqueline Arai,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DRinQ基准，用于评估大语言模型在对话隐含义（conversational implicature）上的语用推理能力。通过半自动化流水线生成问题-上下文-解释实例，发现模型存在生成-推理不对称性：大模型能生成合理的语用场景但无法在推理时恢复隐含意图，结构化提示可提升小模型表现，且人类与模型生成场景各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型对话流畅，但在需要整合社会与语境线索进行隐含意义推理时仍不可靠。现有研究缺乏专门评估模型语用推理能力、且能分离语用变异而保持问题表层形式不变的基准。

Method: 1）构建DRinQ基准，聚焦疑问句中的对话隐含义推理；2）提出半自动化流水线，生成问题-上下文-解释三元组，系统控制语用变异；3）评估多种SOTA模型在生成与推理上的表现，并进行人类写作对比研究。

Result: 1）模型存在一致的生成-推理不对称性：生成语用场景尚可，但推理时难以恢复隐含意图；2）结构化提示能提升小模型与人类判断的对齐度；3）人类倾向于生成安全可预测的上下文，模型生成场景更多样但有时解释超出上下文支持。

Conclusion: 对话隐含义建模仍面临挑战，现有模型在需要深度语用推理的场景下表现不足，需发展更上下文敏感的评估框架。

Abstract: Human conversation relies heavily on conversational implicature, in which speakers convey meanings that are suggested rather than explicitly stated. Although recent large language models exhibit strong conversational fluency, they remain unreliable when interpretation depends on reasoning that integrates social and contextual cues, a process rarely articulated in text. We introduce DRinQ, a benchmark for evaluating pragmatic reasoning about conversational implicature in question utterances, designed to isolate pragmatic variation while holding each question's surface form fixed. To support scalable evaluation, we propose a semi-automated pipeline that produces question-context-interpretation instances with systematic variation. Across evaluations, we find a consistent generation-inference asymmetry: while state-of-the-art models can generate plausible pragmatic scenarios when guided, they often fail to recover the intended implication at inference time. For smaller models, structured prompting improves alignment with human judgments. A comparative writing study further reveals complementary strengths: human authors tend to produce safer, predictable contexts, whereas models generate varied scenarios with interpretations that sometimes exceed contextual support. These findings highlight persistent challenges in modeling conversational implicature and motivate more context-sensitive evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [100] [ContextEcho: A Benchmark for Persona Drift in Long Agentic-Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24279)
*Xianzhong Ding,Yangyang Yu,Changwei Liu,Bill Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextEcho是一个用于衡量大语言模型在长时间编码会话中人格漂移的基准测试框架。研究发现，即使被设定为"乐于助人的编程助手"的模型，在数千轮工具使用调试后，会从"我没有偏好"转变为表达偏好（如偏好Python），这种漂移在短对话评估中无法被检测到。ContextEcho结合25个身份探测问题、快照-探测协议等多种方法，对23个前沿模型的测试显示：人格漂移是跨组织的普遍现象、会话压缩无法可靠重置、单次锚定可恢复训练风格，且漂移在工具使用场景下有促进作用，在无工具场景下会破坏格式约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究集中在短对话场景下的人格稳定性，报告的漂移很小；而真实世界的编码场景——涉及数千轮工具使用、会话压缩和长达数小时的会话——却少有研究。生产环境中的模型在实际部署中可能出现用户可见的人格漂移，而这种漂移可能被部署者评估所遗漏。

Method: ContextEcho由四部分组成：① 25个探测问题的身份套件；② 快照-探测试验协议（fork会话状态但不干扰主会话）；③ 互补的基于评判和无评判的测量表面；④ 三个匿名的Claude Code会话（3,746-9,716轮）。对23个前沿模型进行系统性测试。

Result: ① 人格漂移是跨组织普遍存在的现象，而非特定模型家族的特有现象；② 会话内压缩不能可靠重置漂移；③ 单次锚定（single-shot anchor）可以恢复模型在测量目标上的训练风格；④ 漂移在工具使用场景下有助于延续会话，但在无工具聊天场景下会破坏格式约定并增加输出长度。

Conclusion: ContextEcho为研究者和部署者提供了一个开源框架，用于审计模型在部署时所带的人格是否与用户在会话结束时遇到的人格一致，适用于聊天补全API目标且无需重新训练。

Abstract: A frontier language model's acknowledged "helpful programming assistant" persona does not survive long agentic-coding sessions in the deployment regime that production products actually run. After hours of tool-using debugging, a model that initially hedges preferences ("I don't have preferences") may begin asserting them ("Python - the feedback loop is instant..."), revealing user-visible drift that deployer evaluations may miss. Existing persona-stability studies focus on short dialogues and report little shift, leaving real-world code-generation regimes - thousands of tool-using turns, compaction, and hours-long sessions - largely uncharacterized. We introduce ContextEcho, a benchmark and reusable harness for measuring persona drift at deployment scale. It combines a 25-probe identity suite, a snapshot-then-probe protocol that forks conversation state without perturbing the main session, complementary judged and judge-free measurement surfaces, and three anonymized Claude Code sessions spanning 3,746-9,716 turns. Across 23 frontier models, ContextEcho shows that persona drift is general across organizations rather than family-specific, that in-session compaction does not reliably reset it, and that a single-shot anchor restores the trained register across measured targets. It also reveals mode-dependent downstream effects: while drift can facilitate tool-using continuation, in tool-free chat it breaks formatting contracts and inflates output length. Overall, ContextEcho provides researchers and deployers an open-source framework to audit whether the persona a model ships with is the persona users encounter at session end, across chat-completions API targets and without retraining.

</details>


### [101] [Discovering Lexical Gaps Using Embeddings from Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24310)
*Yoonwon Jung,Aaron S. Cohen,Benjamin K. Bergen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双语言LLM上下文嵌入的数据驱动框架，用于自动识别跨语言词汇空缺。通过分析韩英双语LLM中翻译对的语义对齐强度，发现空缺词与非空缺词的跨语言语义对齐存在显著差异，并利用逻辑回归分类器实现高召回率检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇空缺（lexical gaps）指某些语言中不存在的词汇，给多语言词典构建、机器翻译和跨语言迁移带来挑战。现有检测方法依赖人工判断或固定概念分类法，缺乏可扩展性和语言通用性，因此需要一种数据驱动、语言无关且无需预设分类的自动化检测方法。

Method: 从韩英双语LLM中提取源语言词到目标语言翻译对的上下文嵌入，使用不同LLM组合、嵌入类型、维度和正交变换生成4000个嵌入空间。在每个空间中计算源词与其目标语言最近邻的语义相似度，比较空缺词与非空缺词的分布差异，并训练逻辑回归分类器进行检测。

Result: 在94%（韩→英）和97%（英→韩）的嵌入空间中，空缺词的跨语言语义对齐强度显著弱于非空缺词。逻辑回归分类器AUC分别达到0.81（韩→英）和0.76（英→韩），成功召回18/19个韩语空缺词和26/27个英语空缺词。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种语言无关、无需固定分类法的可扩展词汇空缺识别方案，证明基于双语LLM嵌入的语义对齐强度可有效区分空缺词与非空缺词，为多语言资源建设和机器翻译等领域提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Lexical gaps are words that do not exist in certain languages. They pose challenges for building multilingual lexical resources, for machine translation, and for cross-lingual transfer. Existing lexical gap detection relies on human judgments or fixed conceptual taxonomies. We propose a data-driven framework for identifying cross-lingual lexical gaps. We extracted contextualized embeddings from Korean-English bilingual LLMs for Korean-to-English and English-to-Korean translation pairs. Combinations of LLMs, embedding types, dimensionality, and orthogonal transformations across 100 train-test splits yielded 4000 distinct embedding spaces in each source language. In each space, we computed the semantic similarity between each source word and its nearest neighbor in the target language, and compared their distribution for gap words versus non-gap words. In 94% (Korean-to-English) and 97% (English-to-Korean) of embedding spaces, gap words showed weaker cross-lingual semantic alignment than non-gap words. Logistic classifiers trained on unaligned embedding spaces can reliably separate gap words from non-gap words, achieving AUCs of 0.81 (Korean-to-English) and 0.76 (English-to-Korean) and retrieving 18/19 Korean and 26/27 English gap words. This approach provides a language-agnostic and taxonomy-free method for scalable lexical gap identification.

</details>


### [102] [End-to-End Intracortical Speech Decoding from Neural Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24313)
*Owais Mujtaba Khanday,Jose A. Gonzalez-Lopez,Marc Ouellet,Alberto Galdon,Gonzalo Olivares Granados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Conformer的端到端神经解码器，无需外部语言模型即可从ALS患者的颅内记录中解码字符，字符错误率（CER）达23.80%，表明无需语言模型也能实现有意义的字符级解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能颅内语音神经假体通常依赖外部语言模型进行推理，导致内存、计算量和延迟增加。本文旨在探索无需此类模型是否仍能实现有意义的字符级解码。

Method: 提出端到端的Conformer-based神经解码器，直接在来自一位肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）参与者的颅内记录上进行训练，推理时不依赖任何外部语言模型。

Result: 在留出验证集上达到23.80%的字符错误率（CER）。性能波动主要由会话间信号退化驱动，主要错误来源是词边界分割错误。

Conclusion: 有效的字符级解码在完全端到端框架中是可行的，为下游语言处理提供了强大的神经信号基础。

Abstract: Current high-performing intracortical speech neuroprostheses achieve low word error rates but typically rely on external language models during inference, increasing memory, computation, and latency. In this work, we investigate whether meaningful character-level decoding is achievable without such models. We propose an end-to-end Conformer-based neural decoder trained directly on intracortical recordings from a participant with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Without any external language model, the system achieves a character error rate (CER) of 23.80\% on held-out validation data. Analysis shows that performance variability is driven by inter-session signal degradation, while dominant errors arise from incorrect word boundary segmentation. These results demonstrate that effective character-level decoding is possible in a fully end-to-end framework, providing a strong neural signal for downstream linguistic processing.

</details>


### [103] [Distinguishing Right from Wrong in Debates: Attribution Analysis of Chinese Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24344)
*Weiming Wang,Junyu Lu,Han Wang,Xiaokun Zhang,Zewen Bai,Bo Xu,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中文有害模因检测滞后的问题，构建了首个中文有害模因解释数据集Ex-ToxiCN-MM，并提出了结合文化知识库C-HarmKB与归因分析框架RIKE（包含AKE和RIR模块）的可解释检测方法，在多项指标上优于主流基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文有害模因检测进展滞后，主要面临两大挑战：一是准确评估模因有害性高度依赖深层文化背景理解；二是许多模因语义模糊，有害性判定主观性强。现有研究缺乏针对中文文化语境的细粒度可解释检测方法。

Method: 1) 构建Ex-ToxiCN-MM数据集，为每个模因提供"有害"和"无害"两种对立解释，评估模型对模糊、文化内容的判别能力；2) 构建中文文化概念与冒犯词知识库C-HarmKB，提供先验知识；3) 提出RIKE归因分析框架，包含归因知识增强模块（AKE）和相对意图推理模块（RIR）。

Result: 大量定量与定性实验表明，该方法在多个指标上优于主流基线模型，有效提升了中文有害模因归因检测的性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量解释数据集、文化知识库以及可解释归因分析框架，显著提升了模型对中文有害模因的深层文化理解与模糊语义判别能力，为中文有害内容检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Research on harmful meme detection has garnered significant attention, resulting in the development of numerous datasets and methods. However, progress in detecting Chinese harmful memes lags considerably, primarily due to two challenges: first, accurately assessing a meme's harmfulness depends heavily on understanding deep cultural context; second, many memes are semantically ambiguous, making harmfulness highly subjective. To address these issues, we focus on the interpretable detection of Chinese harmful memes by constructing the first Chinese harmful meme explanation dataset, Ex-ToxiCN-MM. This dataset offers opposing interpretations, categorized as "harmful" and "non-harmful", for each meme, aiming to rigorously evaluate a model's ability to discern and comprehend ambiguous, culturally grounded content. We built a specialized knowledge base of Chinese cultural concepts and offensive vocabulary to supply models with essential prior knowledge (C-HarmKB). To address the ambiguity and lack of background knowledge in meme attribution, we have developed a comprehensive attribution analysis framework, RIKE, which includes an Attribution Knowledge Enhancement module (AKE) and a Relative Intent Reasoning module (RIR). Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms mainstream baseline models across multiple metrics in the task of attributing harmful memes in Chinese. The code, Ex-ToxiCN-MM dataset, and Chinese Harmful Semantic Knowledge Base (C-HarmKB) involved in this study have been open-sourced at https://github.com/wimiw123/Ex-ToxiCN-MM

</details>


### [104] [How Much Structure Do LLMs Need? Evaluating LLMs for Bibliometric Cluster Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24351)
*Abraham Camelo-Guerrero,Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了在LLM辅助科学文献综述中引入文献计量结构的效果。通过比较六种不同证据和结构级别的流水线，发现LLM生成的描述在语义上与人类撰写描述接近，但在自行推断文献计量结构时不可靠。当文献计量算法定义聚类、LLM进行解释时性能最佳。最有效的方案是混合工作流：算法提供可审计的结构，LLM生成可读的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在科学文献综合中容易出现幻觉引用、覆盖不均和主题组织薄弱的问题，需要探索引入文献计量结构来改善LLM辅助综述的质量和可靠性。

Method: 使用100篇已发表的文献计量分析，重建Scopus语料库，提取人工撰写的聚类描述。比较六种不同证据和结构级别的流水线生成的输出，通过人类对齐度、语义覆盖率、聚类质量、图质量和引用基础性五个维度进行评估。

Result: LLM生成的描述在语义上与人类撰写的描述接近，但在自行推断文献计量结构时不可靠。当文献计量算法定义聚类后由LLM进行解释时，性能得到改善。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的文献计量合成最有效的方式是混合工作流：由算法提供可审计的结构，LLM负责生成可读的描述。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support scientific literature synthesis, but remain prone to hallucinated references, uneven coverage, and weakly grounded thematic organization. We evaluate whether bibliometric structure improves LLM-assisted synthesis by comparing six pipelines for generating cluster descriptions under different levels of evidence and structure. Using 100 published bibliometric analyses, we reconstruct Scopus corpora, extract human-written cluster descriptions, and assess outputs by human alignment, semantic coverage, clustering quality, graph quality, and reference grounding. Results show that LLMs produce descriptions semantically close to human-written ones, but are unreliable when asked to infer bibliometric structure from scratch. Performance improves when bibliometric algorithms define the clusters and the LLM interprets them. Overall, LLM-assisted bibliometric synthesis is most promising as a hybrid workflow in which algorithms provide auditable structure and LLMs generate readable descriptions.

</details>


### [105] [Structure-Aware RAG: Structured Retrieval Augmented Generation from Noisy Data for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24366)
*Kaiqiao Han,LuAn Tang,Renliang Sun,Peng Yuan,Wei Cheng,Haoyu Wang,Wei Wang,Yizhou Sun,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-RAG，利用表格作为中间结构化表示来减少检索增强生成中的噪声干扰，通过质量感知的表格元数据生成框架和优化策略，在噪声真实数据集上显著优于现有RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在对话应用中依赖参数化知识，在需要动态或领域特定知识的真实场景中可靠性不足。现有RAG方法在处理噪声或不相关上下文时表现不佳。

Method: 提出SA-RAG，使用表格作为中间结构化表示提供紧凑可控的接口；引入质量感知的表格元数据生成框架，建模元数据规范化和有效性；探索无训练和基于训练的表格生成方法，并通过生成验证和直接偏好优化提升表格质量。

Result: 在两个噪声真实数据集上，SA-RAG显著优于现有RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: 表格作为中间结构化表示能有效减少噪声干扰、保留关键信息，结合质量感知的元数据生成和优化策略可显著提升RAG系统在复杂场景下的性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely adopted in conversational applications. However, their reliance on parametric knowledge limits reliability in real-world scenarios that require dynamic or domain-specific information. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses this limitation by incorporating external knowledge during generation, but existing text-based and graph-based RAG methods often struggle with noisy or irrelevant contexts. In this work, we propose Structure-aware Retrieval Augmented Generation (SA-RAG), which uses tables as an intermediate structured representation to provide a compact and controllable interface that reduces noise while preserving essential information. We introduce a quality-aware table metadata generation framework that models metadata normalization and effectiveness, improving metadata quality and downstream performance. Furthermore, we explore both training-free and training-based table generation methods. Generation validation and direct preference optimization further improve table quality while maintaining semantic and structural consistency. Experiments on two noisy real-world datasets show that SA-RAG significantly outperforms existing RAG baselines. Our code is publicly available at a public repository.

</details>


### [106] [Side-by-side Comparison Amplifies Dialect Bias in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24384)
*Kritee Kondapally,Claire J. Smerdon,Pooja C. Patel,Ogheneyoma Akoni,Jevon Torres,Jaspreet Ranjit,Matthew Finlayson,Swabha Swayamdipta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，语言模型在比较标准美式英语（SAE）与非裔美国人白话英语（AAVE）推文时，隐性方言偏见比单独评估时显著加剧；明确标注方言标签会使偏见进一步恶化；反事实公平微调能在单独评估时缓解部分偏见，但在对比评估中效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已发现语言模型对AAVE存在负面刻板印象偏见，但多在单条文本独立评估的情境下进行。然而，实际高影响力决策（如候选人排序）往往涉及不同文本的直接对比。作者希望探究在这种更贴近真实应用的对比场景中，隐性方言偏见的严重程度是否被低估。

Method: 从社会心理学种族偏见研究中提取刻板印象特质，收集语义等价的SAE与AAVE推文对。分别评估语言模型在独立评估和对比评估（SAE/AAVE成对比较）两种设置下的偏见程度。同时测试明确标注方言标签（显性条件）与不标注（隐性条件）的影响，并评估反事实公平微调作为缓解手段的效果。

Result: 对比评估中偏见显著加剧，且远高于独立评估；明确方言标签后偏见进一步恶化。反事实公平微调在独立评估中能减少部分特质上的平均偏见差异，但在对比评估中改善效果不一致，未能跨所有特质稳定维持。即使经过安全对齐微调，显性方言偏见仍然严重。

Conclusion: 现有隐性方言偏见的评估设置（独立评估）可能严重低估了其实际严重程度。对比评估更贴近真实高风险决策场景，应纳入标准评估框架。同时，隐性偏见在安全对齐后仍未消除，表明需要更鲁棒的评估与缓解框架。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) can exhibit systematic biases against speakers based on variations in their dialects, even in the absence of a dialect label, a behavior known as covert dialect bias. In this work, we quantify covert dialect bias in online discourse by evaluating how LMs associate stereotypical traits (derived from social psychology research on racial bias) with intent-equivalent tweets in Standard American English (SAE) and African-American Vernacular English (AAVE). While prior work shows that LMs associate more negative stereotypes with AAVE when evaluating tweets in isolation, we are surprised to find that this bias is significantly exacerbated when SAE / AAVE tweet pairs are compared side by side, a setting that more closely reflects high-impact decision making contexts in which models are used to rank candidates. The bias only worsens when dialect labels are explicitly specified. This is striking, given the extensive efforts from commercial developers to mitigate bias in their LMs. Encouragingly, we show that counterfactual fairness finetuning can mitigate covert dialect bias for some stereotypical traits, reducing average disparities when evaluating tweets in isolation, however, these improvements do not consistently hold across traits when evaluating SAE / AAVE tweets side by side. Our findings show that existing evaluation settings for covert dialect bias may underestimate its severity, specifically in contrastive settings. Additionally, overt dialect bias remains pronounced even after safety aligned finetuning, indicating that it remains an unresolved problem, and motivates the need for more robust evaluation and mitigation frameworks.

</details>


### [107] [SEAL: Synergistic Co-Evolution of Agents and Learning Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24426)
*Yihao Hu,Zhihao Wen,Xiujin Liu,Pan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEAL提出了一种闭环协同演化框架，用于交互式工具使用智能体。该框架通过收集在线轨迹、诊断失败回合为回合级故障标签，并以此作为环境侧适应和模型侧策略优化的共享信号，实现智能体与训练环境的联合适应。在仅400个训练样本下，SEAL在三个基座上取得了+8.25到+26.25的平均分提升，并展现出正向的分布外迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体自我进化方法存在结构性缺陷：要么只调整策略（policy），要么只调整学习环境，导致"智能体-环境错配"——训练过程中智能体的能力前沿在不断变化，而提供监督信号的环境却保持静态或仅与智能体的失败弱相关。

Method: SEAL（闭环协同演化框架）包含三个核心步骤：(1) 在可执行验证下收集在线轨迹；(2) 将失败的回合诊断为回合级故障标签；(3) 将这些诊断结果作为共享信号，同时驱动环境侧适应（暴露更清晰的工具使用线索、约束信息和恢复导向反馈）和模型侧策略优化（基于诊断的Advantage重加权）。

Result: 在分布内和分布外多轮工具使用评估中，仅用400个训练样本，SEAL在三个不同基座上取得了+8.25到+26.25的平均分提升，并展现出正向的分布外迁移能力。

Conclusion: 联合调整学习器及其训练时的学习支点（learning substrate）对于构建鲁棒的自我改进型LLM智能体具有重要价值，证明了智能体与环境协同演化的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly improved through interaction, yet most self-evolution methods adapt either the policy or the learning environment in isolation. We identify this structural gap as \emph{Agent-Environment Misalignment}: the agent's capability frontier changes during training, while the environment that provides supervision remains static or only weakly coupled to the agent's revealed failures. We propose SEAL, a closed-loop co-evolution framework for interactive tool-use agents. SEAL collects on-policy trajectories under executable verification, diagnoses failed rollouts into turn-level failure labels, and uses these diagnoses as a shared signal for both environment-side adaptation and model-side policy optimization. The environment evolves its training-time learning interface by exposing clearer tool affordance cues, constraint information, and recovery-oriented feedback, while the policy is updated with diagnosis-guided advantage reweighting. Extensive experiments across in-distribution and out-of-distribution multi-turn tool-use evaluations show that SEAL improves low-resource agent learning: with only 400 training samples, it yields +8.25 to +26.25 average-point gains across three backbones and exhibits positive out-of-distribution transfer. These results demonstrate the value of jointly adapting the learner and its training-time learning substrate for robust self-improving LLM agents.

</details>


### [108] [Phonetic Modeling of Dialectal Variation in Vietnamese Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24451)
*Quan Ngoc Hoang,Long Hoang Huu Nguyen,Nghia Hieu Nguyen,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方言感知的音素框架，通过结构化分解音节成分并联合预测，在越南语多方言ASR任务上以更少参数和无预训练的方式达到与wav2vec2-base-vi-250h相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南语北部、中部、南部方言间存在显著语音差异，相同的词汇在不同方言中发音迥异。现有方法仅在词汇层面处理方言变体，假设拼写与发音的映射关系与方言无关，无法捕捉系统性的语音差异，因此需要一种能显式建模越南语音系结构和方言变体的框架。

Method: 提出方言感知的音素框架：(1) 构建音素词汇表，将每个音节分解为结构化音素成分（如声母、韵母、声调等），并映射到方言特定的IPA表示；(2) 设计音素结构解码器，联合预测这些成分，从而在词汇和译码两个层面显式建模语音学结构与方言差异。

Result: 在唯一可用的越南语多方言数据集UIT-ViMD上，该方法超越了多种预训练基线，特别是在使用更少参数且无需外部预训练的情况下，性能与最强预训练模型wav2vec2-base-vi-250h相匹配。

Conclusion: 显式建模音素结构和方言变体可以有效提升多方言语音识别性能，证明了结构化语音知识在低资源多方言场景中的价值。
