Method: 定义了幽默推理数据对象，设计并迭代优化提示策略，促使LLM生成对普通人群有效的幽默解释，从早期提示改进到后期版本以减少关键错误，并大规模生成数据对象。

Result: 改进后的提示显著减少了关键错误，生成了大量可用于数据合成与增强的幽默数据对象，提升了幽默解释质量。

Conclusion: 更好的LLM提示策略能提升幽默解释质量，尤其在处理缺失上下文、多模态和转录问题方面更为细致，为AI理解幽默作为社会行为的未来研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: In most existing AI humor research, humor was treated as either "present" or "not present." We explore the concept of humor as a social interaction with context and explanations. During this project, we defined a humor reasoning data object and developed a way to prompt LLMs to generate an explanation of humor effective for general population. We iterated from an earlier prompt to an improved prompt, found that the later version reduced important errors, and then scaled generation to a large number of data objects which have the potential to enable data synthesis and data augmentation for AI humor research. Our main takeaway is that better prompting of an LLM improves humor explanation quality, especially by handling missing context, multi-modality, and transcript issues more carefully. These results establish a strong foundation for future work on AI understanding of humor as social behavior.

</details>


### [146] [Locality Matters for Training-Free Audio Token Compression in Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25179)
*Jiale Luo,Xiaoyu Liang,Haoji Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LTBM，一种训练免费的无编码器空间音频令牌压缩方法，通过局部时序二分合并（局部性感知）降低音频-语言模型推理成本。实验表明，局部性合并更有利于音频字幕生成，而全局匹配更适合多选音频理解任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-语言模型（ALMs）中，音频输入作为长前缀令牌序列，消耗上下文预算、增加内存使用，在资源受限或延迟敏感场景下难以部署。现有训练免费的令牌缩减方法（固定池化、基于分数的剪枝）存在内容无关或丢弃邻近声学上下文的问题。

Method: 提出Local Temporal Bipartite Merging (LTBM)，一种训练免费的编码器空间压缩方法，在显式时间窗口约束下合并相似邻近音频令牌。同时设置受控的Global Merge变体以隔离时序局部性是否为有用归纳偏置。

Result: 在AudioCaps、Clotho和MMAU上使用Qwen2-Audio实验，发现任务相关的局部性效应：局部性合并更有利于字幕生成（尤其强压缩下），全局匹配更有利于多选音频理解。在Audio Flamingo 3上的交叉骨干验证进一步支持了局部性合并的字幕生成优势。

Conclusion: 时序局部性是音频令牌压缩中有用的归纳偏置，但效果依赖于任务类型：字幕生成受益于局部性，音频理解受益于全局性。LTBM为训练免费压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Audio-language models (ALMs) are increasingly used for audio captioning, question answering, and open-ended audio understanding, but their inference cost remains high when audio inputs are represented as long prefix-token sequences. These audio prefixes consume context budget, increase memory usage, and make deployment harder in resource-constrained or latency-sensitive settings. Existing training-free audio-token reduction methods mainly rely on fixed pooling or score-based pruning. Fixed pooling is content-agnostic, while score-based pruning can preserve isolated salient tokens but discard nearby acoustic context. We propose Local Temporal Bipartite Merging (LTBM), a training-free encoder-space compression method that merges similar nearby audio tokens under an explicit temporal window constraint. Beyond introducing LTBM, we use a controlled Global Merge variant to isolate whether temporal locality itself is a useful inductive bias for audio-token compression. Experiments on AudioCaps, Clotho, and MMAU with Qwen2-Audio show evidence of a task-dependent locality effect: locality-aware merging is more favorable for captioning at several compression settings, especially under stronger compression, while global matching is more competitive for multiple-choice audio understanding. A cross-backbone validation on Audio Flamingo 3 further supports the captioning-side advantage of locality-aware merging under moderate and aggressive compression.

</details>


### [147] [By Their Fruits You Will Know Them: Comparing Formalizations of Law by the Decisions They Encode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25186)
*Julius Vernie,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种系统方法，用于比较同一法律条款的不同形式化表示，通过SAT求解器枚举边缘案例并用自然语言生成具体事实场景，供法律专家审查。该方法应用于9个前沿LLM对10项欧盟条款的形式化结果，发现行为分歧与结构一致性几乎不相关，且生成的案例揭示了多种定性分歧，其中一些反映了法律评论中的真实争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律条款的形式化有望实现机器可访问的法律和自动法律推理，但任何形式化都隐含着难以预料的解释性选择，尤其当LLM作为形式化作者时。现有方法缺乏对不同形式化方案的系统性比较，无法揭示它们在实际案例推理中的分歧。

Method: 给定同一法律条款的多个形式化表示后，在节点层级对其进行匹配，从匹配结果中为每对形式化推导共享接口，然后使用SAT求解器枚举任意两个形式化之间存在分歧的边缘案例，最后将选中的边缘案例转化为具体事实场景，供法律专家审查和决策。

Result: 对9个前沿LLM生成的10项欧盟条款形式化进行分析，发现形式化之间的行为分歧与结构一致性几乎不相关；生成的案例揭示了定性的分歧类型，包括反映法律评论中真实争议的分歧。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效揭示不同LLM形式化方案之间的隐含分歧，为法律人工智能中的形式化质量评估和司法解释差异分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Formalizing legal provisions promises machine-accessible law and automated legal reasoning, and recent LLMs make it tempting to generate such formalizations directly from statutory text. However, any formalization makes implicit interpretive choices whose consequences are hard to anticipate, especially if an LLM is the author. We present a method for systematically comparing different formalizations of the same legal provision by their inferences on individual cases. Given multiple formalizations of a provision, we match them at the node level, derive a shared interface for each pair from the matching, and use a SAT solver to enumerate the edge cases on which any two formalizations disagree. Selected edge cases are then verbalized into concrete factual scenarios that a legal expert can examine and act on. We apply our method to formalizations of ten EU provisions generated by nine frontier LLMs. We find that behavioral divergence between formalizations is essentially uncorrelated with their structural agreement and that the verbalized cases reveal qualitatively distinct types of disagreement, including divergences that mirror genuine controversies in the legal commentary.

</details>


### [148] [GroupTravelBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Multi-Person Travel Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25200)
*Xiang Cheng,Yulan Hu,Lulu Zheng,Zheng Pan,Xin Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GroupTravelBench 是一个面向多用户、多轮次旅行规划的基准测试，通过评估LLM智能体在偏好 elicitation、冲突协调和组规划三方面的能力，揭示现有模型在偏好覆盖和群体公平性方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准仅考虑单用户场景，忽略了真实世界中多用户间的冲突识别与解决这一关键挑战。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能评估LLM智能体在多用户、多轮对话中规划旅行能力的基准。

Method: 基于真实用户画像、POI数据和票价数据，合成650个任务并按难度分为三级。构建交互式沙盒环境（含缓存真实工具数据），评估LLM在elicitation（主动多轮对话收集偏好）、coordination（妥协或分组策略解决冲突）、planning（搜索最大化群体效用且兼顾公平可行的方案）三方面的能力。

Result: 即使是最前沿的LLM模型，在偏好覆盖和群体公平性方面仍表现出显著弱点。

Conclusion: GroupTravelBench 为推进面向真实旅行规划的LLM智能体研究提供了一个实用且可复现的基准，揭示了当前模型在多用户协作规划场景下的关键不足。

Abstract: Travel planning is a realistic task for evaluating the planning and tool-use abilities of LLM agents. However, existing benchmarks typically assume only a single user, thereby avoiding one of the most challenging aspects of real-world scenarios: an agent's ability to identify and resolve conflicts among multiple users. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{GroupTravelBench}, the first benchmark for \textbf{multi-user, multi-turn} travel planning. Based on real user profiles, POI data, and ticket price data, we synthesize 650 tasks and divide them into three difficulty levels. Beyond standard abilities in single-user itinerary planning, such as multi-step reasoning and tool use, our benchmark further evaluates three key capabilities required for travel agents: \emph{(i) elicitation} -- proactively engaging in multi-turn dialogue to gather preferences from each user; \emph{(ii) coordination} -- resolving conflicts among users through compromise or subgrouping strategies; and \emph{(iii) planning} -- searching for travel plans that maximize overall group utility while maintaining fairness and feasibility. To simulate real-world conversational itinerary planning while enabling reliable tool use and offline evaluation, we build an interactive sandbox environment with cached real-world tool data. We evaluate a wide range of LLMs and find that even frontier models still show substantial weaknesses in preference coverage and group fairness. \textit{GroupTravelBench} provides a practical and reproducible benchmark for advancing research on LLM agents for real-world travel planning.

</details>


### [149] [Clarification Is Not Enough: Post-Clarification Answering Remains the Bottleneck in Multi-Turn QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25204)
*Jinyan Su,Jennifer Healey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮问答中的偏好 elicitation 问题，将其分解为澄清策略（决定是提问还是直接回答）和澄清后回答（获取缺失信息后生成最终答案）两个组件。在PACIFIC基准上发现，监督微调能快速提升澄清策略，但最终答案准确率仍然显著偏低，表明理解并正确解释用户回应是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元化对齐要求系统适应多样化的用户价值观、沟通风格和上下文假设，而实现这种对齐的基础前提是在用户意图不明确或模糊时，能够准确进行偏好 elicitation（偏好引导/偏好获取）。

Method: 将多轮问答中的偏好 elicitation 问题分解为两个组件：1) 澄清策略（clarification policy）——决定是否需要追问澄清问题还是直接回答；2) 澄清后回答（post-clarification answering）——在获取缺失信息后生成正确的最终答案。使用PACIFIC基准进行评估。

Result: 监督微调可以快速改善澄清策略，但即使模型采取了正确的行动（追问），最终答案的准确率仍然显著低于预期，存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 理解和正确解释用户的回应是多轮问答系统中的关键瓶颈，仅靠改进澄清策略不足以实现有效的偏好 elicitation。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment requires systems to adapt to diverse user values, communication styles, and contextual assumptions. We believe that a foundational prerequisite for such alignment enabling accurate preference elicitation from people when their intent is under-specified or ambiguous. We study the problem of preference elicitation in multi-turn question answering by decomposing the problem into two components: a \textbf{clarification policy}, which decides whether to ask a clarifying question or answer directly, and \textbf{post-clarification answering}, which produces the correct final answer once the missing information is provided. We show, using the PACIFIC benchmark, that supervised fine-tuning rapidly improves the clarification policy, however, final answer accuracy remains substantially lower even when the model takes the correct action. This gap indicates that understanding and correctly interpreting the user's response is the critical gap in multi-turn question-answering systems.

</details>


### [150] [They Are Not the Same: Direct Causes Are Not Grounded Emotion Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25208)
*Zhuangzhuang Pan,Yan Xia,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出情感-原因对提取（ECPE）任务常被简化为二元对/非对预测，但这仅能提取直接触发原因，无法提供基于证据的合理解释。实验表明，二元边界无法稳定容纳情感上下文（emo-context），且模型在短路径压力下更倾向于便利归因而非扎实解释，高ECPE性能并不代表模型真正解释了情感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECPE任务旨在解释情感为何发生，但现有方法将其简化为二元对/非对预测，这种简化是否足以实现情感解释的原始目标值得质疑。

Method: 在IEMO-MECP数据集上分析原始正负样本在二元任务中的保留比例，并引入情感上下文（emo-context）概念，考察二元边界对这类证据的支持情况，同时评估现有ECPE模型对直接触发器和上下文支持的恢复能力，以及短路径压力下的模型行为。

Result: 90.9%的原始正样本仍为情感-原因对，95.0%的原始负样本仍为非对；情感上下文出现在二元边界两侧，且在边界附近富集；现有模型对直接触发器的恢复优于上下文支持；在短路径压力下，二元训练模型更倾向给词汇相似的非对候选分配高分。

Conclusion: 高二元ECPE性能仅表明模型能识别直接触发，不代表模型已真正解释情感；二元边界无法为情感上下文提供稳定位置，模型存在便利归因偏差。

Abstract: Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction (ECPE) was introduced to explain why an emotion occurs, but this goal is now often reduced to binary pair/non-pair prediction. This proxy is useful for direct-cause extraction, yet easy to over-read as evidence grounded emotion explanation. We show that this interpretation is only partially valid. In IEMO-MECP, 90.9% of original positives remain emo-cause and 95.0% of original negatives remain non-pair, confirming that the binary ECPE task is largely preserved. The problem is that direct triggers alone do not constitute a grounded explanation. Emo-context, an utterance that helps interpret a target emotion without directly causing it, appears on both sides of the original boundary and is enriched near binary uncertainty, showing that the binary boundary has no stable place for such discourse evidence. Across evaluated ECPE models, direct triggers are recovered more reliably than contextual support. Under shortcut pressure, this imbalance becomes consequential. Binary-trained models assign higher pair scores to nearby lexically similar non-pair candidates than to evidence supported but structurally harder emo-cause and emo-context pairs. Thus, pair scores can reward convenient attributions over grounded explanations. High binary ECPE performance indicates that a model can identify direct triggers; it does not indicate that the model has explained the emotion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/panzhzh/ECPExsame.

</details>


### [151] [From Automation to Collaboration: Human-in-the-Loop Methods for Safe and Trustworthy NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25226)
*Most. Sharmin Sultana Samu,MD. Tanvir Ahmed Seum,Md. Rakibul Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 综述了人机协同方法在提升大语言模型安全性与可信性方面的应用，涵盖审计、鲁棒性评估、数据构建和模型引导，并指出当前在可扩展探测、可持续基准、低资源场景及私有系统治理方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险任务中广泛部署，但存在偏见、幻觉、对抗攻击和不可靠泛化等风险；人工监督虽关键但成本高且难以规模化，因此需要人机协同方法来提升模型的安全性和可信性。

Method: 对近期人机协同方法进行系统性综述，从审计（基于探测的模型行为不一致性检查）、鲁棒性评估（对抗性文本生成，尤其关注低资源语言）、数据构建（特定领域标注）和模型引导四个维度展开分析。

Result: 发现可扩展探测、可持续鲁棒性基准、低资源语言环境下的评估以及私有系统治理方面仍存在显著空白。企业级Text-to-SQL场景中，在私有大规模数据库上验证输出极为困难。

Conclusion: 指出了未来研究方向：自适应审计、协同评估和可问责部署，强调从自动化转向人机协作是提升LLM安全性与可信性的关键路径。

Abstract: Large language models are widely deployed in high-stakes NLP tasks, yet risks such as bias, hallucination, adversarial vulnerability and unreliable generalization remain. Probe-based auditing reveals inconsistencies in model behavior. Adversarial text generation uncovers robustness gaps, especially in lower-resourced languages with limited benchmarks. Enterprise text-to-SQL settings expose the difficulty of validating outputs over private and large-scale databases. Human supervision is essential for probe validation, adversarial verification and domain-specific annotation, but it is costly and hard to scale. This survey examines recent human-in-the-loop methods that shift NLP from automation toward collaboration for safety and trustworthiness. We review how human expertise supports auditing, robustness evaluation, data construction and model steering. Our findings highlight gaps in scalable probing, sustainable robustness benchmarks, low-resource settings and governance of private systems. We outline practical research directions for adaptive auditing, collaborative evaluation and accountable deployment.

</details>


### [152] [JudgmentBench: Comparing Rubric and Preference Evaluation for Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25240)
*Russell Yang,Ruishi Chen,Pierce Kelaita,Riya Ranjan,Sibo Ma,Charles Dickens,Matthew Guillod,Megan Ma,Julian Nyarko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布JudgmentBench基准，通过从执业律师处收集的1,539个评分和1,530个成对偏好判断，比较了基于评分标准和比较判断两种评估方法，发现比较判断在恢复预期质量排序方面显著优于评分标准（斯皮尔曼秩相关系数0.908 vs 0.150），且所需标注时间不到后者的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试中广泛使用基于评分标准的评分法和比较判断法，但两者之间的选择很少得到实证论证，缺乏在同一高专业领域内由同一专家对同一项目同时收集两种监督信号的数据集来进行系统比较。

Method: 构建JudgmentBench基准，包含30个真实法律任务，从执业律师处收集1,539个评分标准和1,530个成对偏好判断；使用LLM生成三个质量层次的输出，比较两种方法在恢复预期质量排序方面的表现（斯皮尔曼秩相关），同时对比人工标注者和LLM自动评分器。

Result: 比较判断在恢复预期质量排序上显著优于评分标准（斯皮尔曼秩相关0.908 vs 0.150，估计差异0.758 [0.494, 1.021]），且标注时间不到后者的一半；该模式在人类标注者和LLM自动评分器中均成立。

Conclusion: 比较判断法在高质量专家领域中是更有效的评估方法，建议在无可验证真实值的领域中应更广泛地采用该方法；该数据集的配对结构为研究专家判断的 elicitation、聚合及监督使用提供了新的研究平台。

Abstract: Two methodologies dominate current practices of benchmarking: rubric-based scoring evaluates items against predefined criteria, whereas comparative judgment elicits pairwise preferences between outputs. Although both methodologies are widely used, the choice between them is rarely justified. We release JudgmentBench, a benchmark of 30 real-world legal tasks, paired with 1,539 rubric scores and 1,530 pairwise preference judgments collected from practicing attorneys--including at major U.S. law firms--with substantial experience. The annotations constitute the first publicly available dataset in a high-expertise domain in which both supervision signals are elicited from the same experts on the same items. Using LLM-generated outputs at three constructed quality levels, we provide an initial empirical comparison: comparative judgments recover the intended quality ordering substantially better than rubrics (mean Spearman's rank correlation of 0.908 vs. 0.150, estimated difference = 0.758 [0.494, 1.021]) while requiring less than half the annotation time. The patterns hold for human annotators and LLM autograders. Beyond this initial comparison, the paired structure of the dataset supports a broader research agenda on how expert judgment should be elicited, aggregated, and used as supervision in domains without verifiable ground truth.

</details>


### [153] [Inference Time Optimization with Confidence Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25244)
*Yu Wang,Minghao Liu,Jiayun Wang,Jinrui Huang,Ankit Shah,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次发现LLM推理轨迹中正确与错误答案的置信度变化模式差异，并提出基于置信度动态增益（CDG）的投票方法，在多个基准测试上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时优化技术（如重复采样）虽已显著提升LLM推理能力，但模型不确定性在这些优化策略中的关键作用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出置信度动态增益（CDG）投票方法，通过追踪推理链中置信度轨迹的演变模式，利用正确回答随时间置信度提升（正增益）而错误回答置信度衰减的独特现象，来改进答案选择。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1、gpt-oss、Gemma-3、Qwen-QwQ四种开源架构上，针对AIME24/25、HMMT25、BRUMO25基准测试，CDG相比基线方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: CDG方法为LLM推理中的答案选择提供了鲁棒的判别信号，并且论文为该现象提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: Inference time optimization techniques, such as repeated sampling, have significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the critical role of model uncertainty remains largely underexplored in these optimization strategies. In this paper, we investigate the dynamics of confidence along reasoning trajectories and for first time reveal a surprising and unique pattern: correct answer traces tend to exhibit confidence improvement over time (positive confidence gain), while incorrect traces show attenuated or declining confidence as reasoning proceeds. Based on this observation, we propose Confidence Dynamic Gain (CDG) based voting, which incorporates how the confidence trajectory of the response evolves along the reasoning chain. Experiments across four open-source architectures (DeepSeek-R1, gpt-oss, Gemma-3, Qwen-QwQ) on the AIME24/25, HMMT25, and BRUMO25 benchmarks demonstrate that CDG yields a significant performance boost over baselines. These results demonstrate that our method provides a robust discriminative signal for improving answer selection in LLM reasoning. We also provide theoretical insights for this phenomenon. Code will be released at https://github.com/Accenture/CDG.git.

</details>


### [154] [Mimir: Large-scale Multilingual Concept Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25263)
*Elio Musacchio,Lucia Siciliani,Pierpaolo Basile*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mimir，一个1.6B参数的多语言大型概念模型，通过将语言建模从"下一个token预测"转向"下一个概念预测"，改变输入粒度从细粒度token到宽泛概念，并在涵盖46种语言的38亿句子预训练语料和35种语言的6681万指令微调数据上进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究开始质疑基于token的语言模型如何处理和理解语义，以及采用更高粒度是否能够推动该领域发展，因此提出概念建模（Concept Modeling）范式。

Method: 提出Mimir模型（1.6B参数），将输入从token改为概念，训练模型进行下一个概念预测（next-concept prediction）而非下一个token预测（next-token prediction），使用大规模多语言预训练语料（388亿句子，46种语言）和多轮多语言指令微调数据集（6681万句子，35种语言）。

Result: 在同等参数量级下，将Mimir与语言模型进行全面性能对比评估。

Conclusion: 概念建模通过提升输入粒度层次，有望推动语言建模领域的发展，Mimir作为概念模型在多语言理解和生成方面展现出竞争力。

Abstract: Current language modeling approaches are built around tokens. Text corpora are split into tokens, and models are trained by performing computations on these tokens, such as predicting the next token given the preceding ones as context. This paradigm has become the standard in modern language modeling, especially given the outstanding performance obtained by token-based architectures. However, recent works have not only begun to question how language models process and understand meaning from tokens, but also to question whether using higher levels of granularity could advance the research field. This led to the idea of Concept Modeling, that is, to directly train models for next-concept prediction rather than next-token prediction. The goal is to change the input from tokens to concepts, forcing the underlying language model to shift its granularity from fine-grained tokens to broad concepts. In this work, we introduce Mimir, a 1.6B Large Concept Model trained for multilingual concept understanding and generation. We leverage a large-scale multilingual pre-training corpus (38,883,987,240 sentences) spanning 46 languages and a large-scale multi-turn and multilingual instruction-tuning dataset (66,816,428 sentences) covering a total of 35 languages. We extensively evaluate model performance against a language model with a comparable number of parameters.

</details>


### [155] [READER: Reasoning-Enhanced AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25281)
*Pingfan Su,Kai Ye,Shijin Gong,Erhan Xu,Jin Zhu,Giulia Livieri,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: READER是一个仅1.5B参数、结合结构化推理的AI文本检测器，通过微调LLM在REAL监督集上训练，在分布偏移下持续超越GPT-5.2等大规模基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测器在分布偏移下性能严重下降，且缺乏可解释性，难以区分人类与AI生成内容。

Method: 提出REAL数据集（包含人工标注的推理依据和判定标签），在此基础上微调LLM得到READER检测器，使其在推理时先生成结构化推理依据再输出判定结果。

Result: 仅1.5B参数的READER在多个检测任务上持续超越现有检测器以及100-1000倍规模的大型语言模型（GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2）。

Conclusion: 结合结构化推理的小模型可以在AI文本检测任务上以极低的参数量超越超大模型，验证了推理增强策略在检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made it increasingly difficult to distinguish human-written text from AI-generated content. Many existing detectors train supervised neural classifiers that achieve strong in-distribution performance but are often opaque and can degrade substantially under distribution shift. We present READER, a reasoning-enhanced AI text detector that outputs both a human/AI label and a structured rationale describing the evidence for its decision. A key component of our approach is READ, a curated supervision set of rationales and verdicts. We fine-tune an LLM on READ to build READER, which reasons before detecting at inference time. Despite having only 1.5B parameters, READER consistently outperforms existing detectors as well as prompted, high-capacity LLM baselines (GPT-5.2, Gemini-3-Pro, and DeepSeek-V3.2), which are 100 to 1000 times larger in scale.

</details>


### [156] [Knowing but Not Showing: LLMs Recognize Ambiguity but Rarely Ask Clarifying Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25284)
*Jinyan Su,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 尽管模型能识别模糊问题，但在问答中仍倾向直接回答而非主动澄清；检索上下文会进一步加剧这一行为-认知差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户查询常存在歧义，理想助手应主动提出澄清问题而非默认假设用户意图。现有模型是否能识别歧义并采取澄清行为尚不清楚。

Method: 在模糊、清晰和消歧后的问题上，从三个维度评估模型：(1)标准问答；(2)显式歧义判断；(3)行为分析（用评判模型将回答分类为直接回答、拒绝或澄清问题）。

Result: 模型在显式歧义判断任务中能较好识别模糊问题，但在问答场景中几乎总是直接回答；检索到的上下文进一步提高了可回答性，却使模型更少提出澄清问题。

Conclusion: 模型存在识别与行为之间的显著差距：能识别歧义但不会主动澄清，检索上下文反而加剧了这一问题。

Abstract: User queries are often underspecified and may admit multiple valid interpretations. Rather than silently making assumptions about the user's intent, a helpful assistant should surface such ambiguity by asking a clarifying question. Doing so requires two abilities: recognizing that a query is ambiguous, and acting on that recognition by seeking clarification instead of answering directly. To study these abilities, we evaluate models on ambiguous, unambiguous, and disambiguated questions in three settings: standard question answering, explicit ambiguity judgment, and behavioral analysis, where a judge model classifies responses as direct answers, refusals, or clarifying questions. We find a clear gap between recognition and behavior: models often identify ambiguity when explicitly asked to judge it, yet in the QA setting they overwhelmingly default to direct answers. Retrieved context further widens this gap by improving answerability while making models even less likely to ask clarifying questions.

</details>


### [157] [P1SCO: Social Dimensions from a Perspectivist Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25312)
*Amanda Cercas Curry,Gianmarco de Francisci Morales,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: P1SCO是一个跨三个社交媒体平台收集的评论数据集，按十个社会维度进行标注，并包含标注者的人口统计信息、大五人格和政治倾向等元数据，支持多层级分析社会感知的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交评论数据集缺乏对社会维度的系统标注，且忽视了个体差异（如人格、政治倾向）和平台差异对社会感知的影响，需要构建一个同时包含评论层和标注者层信息的数据集来支持更细粒度的分析。

Method: 从三个不同社交平台收集评论，按十个社会维度进行人工标注；同时收集标注者的人口统计信息、大五人格特质和政治倾向，并保留标注者个体视角的多样性，支持评论级、标注者级和平台级的多层级分析。

Result: 构建了一个精心拆解的数据集，允许分析个体评论、标注者和平台三个层级；包含丰富的社会维度标签和标注者元数据，能够支持标注者间/内一致性、人格和政治倾向对社会解读的影响、以及跨平台社交话语动态等研究。

Conclusion: P1SCO数据集通过整合评论级标注与标注者级特征，为研究社交感知在平台、个体差异和人口统计因素间的变化提供了数据基础，有助于深入理解社会话语的跨平台动态。

Abstract: We introduce P1SCO, a dataset of social media comments collected from three distinct platforms, annotated according to ten social dimensions to capture the diversity of social interactions and perceptions. The dataset is carefully disaggregated to allow analysis at the level of individual comments, annotators, and platforms. In addition to the social dimension labels, we include rich metadata on the annotators, including demographics, Big Five personality profiles, and political affiliation. This combination of comment-level annotations and annotator-level features enables nuanced analyses of how social perception varies across platforms, individual differences, and demographic factors. By preserving the diversity of annotator perspectives, our dataset supports studies of inter- and intra-annotator agreement, the influence of personality and political orientation on social interpretation, and the cross-platform dynamics of social discourse.

</details>


### [158] [MATO: Multi-objective Personalized Alignment with Test-time Optimization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25342)
*Linhao Luo,Thuy-Trang Vu,Van-Anh Nguyen,Junae Kim,Gholamreza Haffari,Dinh Phung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MATO是一个无需训练的测试时优化框架，通过从骨干LLM中恢复偏好奖励并动态调整目标权重，在解码过程中实现多目标个性化对齐，实验表明该方法在帕累托改进和可操控性上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖昂贵训练或预训练奖励模型，难以适应动态变化的用户偏好；纯提示方法可操控性有限，无法在冲突目标间可靠地控制相对重要性，导致对齐效果欠佳。

Method: 提出MATO框架：(1) 奖励发现模块从骨干LLM本身恢复自然语言指定的多种偏好奖励；(2) 权重优化模块根据用户初始偏好和部分生成结果动态调整目标权重；(3) 在解码过程中联合使用奖励和权重对token分布进行在线优化。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干LLM上，MATO一致优于强基线方法，实现了帕累托改进的多目标对齐和更强的可操控性。

Conclusion: 测试时优化是走向可扩展、可控且模型无关的个性化对齐的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with diverse and multifaceted user preferences is a fundamental challenge in personalized AI systems. Existing multi-objective alignment methods either rely on costly training or require pre-trained reward models for each preference, making it difficult for them to adapt to evolving preferences. Prompt-based personalization offers a training-free alternative, but prompting alone often provides limited steerability, as LLMs may overemphasize or overlook certain preferences and fail to give users reliable control over the relative importance of different objectives when conflicts arise, leading to suboptimal alignment. In this paper, we introduce MATO, a training-free framework for Multi-objective personalized Alignment with Test-time Optimization. MATO formulates personalization as a test-time optimization problem that steers the relative importance of multiple objectives through controllable weights during decoding, without modifying model parameters or requiring external reward models. Specifically, a reward discovery module recovers preference rewards directly from the backbone LLM for diverse objectives specified in natural language, while a weight optimization module dynamically adjusts objective weights based on the user's initial preferences and the partially generated response to balance competing objectives during generation. The resulting rewards and weights jointly guide an online optimization procedure over the token distribution, enabling better alignment with the target objectives. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs show that MATO consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving Pareto-improving multi-objective alignment and stronger steerability. These results highlight test-time optimization as a promising direction for scalable, controllable, and model-agnostic personalized alignment.

</details>


### [159] [A general tensor-structured compression scheme for efficient large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25344)
*Ying Lu,Peng-Fei Zhou,Qi-Xuan Fang,Pan Zhang,Shi-Ju Ran,Gang Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor Mixture (MixT)，一种通用张量结构压缩方案，用可执行张量算子混合替代密集线性层，在Qwen3-8B和LLaMA2-7B上实现47.5%参数减少、37.1%推理FLOPs降低，同时保持MMLU精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs中密集线性变换带来巨大的存储、内存和计算开销，阻碍高效适配和部署，且掩盖了结构简化的功能影响。现有压缩方法缺乏通用性，未充分利用张量结构。

Method: 提出MixT：一种张量结构压缩方案，将目标密集线性层替换为可直接执行的多张量算子混合（tensor operators mixture），直接作用于通用线性投影而非特定模型组件，具有跨Transformer架构的通用性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和LLaMA2-7B上验证：存在广泛的压缩可行区域，MMLU精度在达到特定模型边界前基本保持不变。在LLaMA2-7B边界处，全模型参数减少47.5%，推理FLOPs降低37.1%，训练FLOPs降低52.1%，峰值推理内存降低60.4%。

Conclusion: MixT展示了张量结构压缩在LLMs中的实用潜力，在模型特定边界处实现大幅压缩同时保持性能，且输出熵、预测熵和层间几何的协同变化揭示了压缩过程中的关键转变机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are dominated by dense linear transformations, whose storage, memory and computational overheads hinder efficient adaptation and deployment while masking the functional impacts of structural simplification. Here we present Tensor Mixture (MixT), a general tensor-structured compression scheme that replaces targeted dense linear layers with natively executable mixtures of tensor operators. Operating directly on generic linear projections instead of model-specific components, MixT is potentially applicable across Transformer-based LLMs and other dense neural mappings. We evaluate MixT on Qwen3-8B and LLaMA2-7B under a unified recovery protocol, identifying a broad compressible regime in which MMLU accuracy is largely preserved before an abrupt transition at model-specific boundaries. This transition coincides with coordinated shifts in output entropy, prediction entropy and inter-layer geometry. At the LLaMA2-7B transition boundary, MixT reduces full-model parameters by 47.5\%, inference FLOPs by 37.1\%, training FLOPs by 52.1\% and peak inference memory by 60.4\%, demonstrating its practical potential for lower-cost LLM compression.

</details>


### [160] [AI-Associated Lexical Shifts Across 34 Languages: Cross-Lingual Convergence and Diachronic Uptake in News Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25358)
*Thomas Stephan Juzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将AI相关词汇偏移的研究从科学英语扩展到WMT新闻语料库中的34种语言，发现AI过度使用的词汇存在显著的跨语言语义趋同现象，且在ChatGPT发布后，26/34种语言的AI过度使用词汇频率显著上升（平均+15.1%），表明AI可能对全球语言使用产生跨语言的同质化压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注科学英语中AI相关的词汇偏移，但尚不清楚这种现象是否存在于其他语言以及是否具有跨语言一致性。本文旨在将这一研究扩展到多种语言，探究AI对全球语言使用的同质化影响。

Method: 使用WMT新闻语料库中的34种语言，采用改进的分半延续诊断方法，比较GPT-4.1的续写与匹配的人类黄金标准文本。通过对数流行率比（log prevalence ratios）推导每种语言中AI过度使用的词位排名，并结合嵌入分析和人工分析验证跨语言语义趋同模式。同时进行ChatGPT发布前后的历时分析，跟踪每种语言前20个AI过度使用词汇的频率变化。

Result: 跨语言语义趋同显著：'emphasize'类动词出现在24/34种语言中。26/34种语言在ChatGPT发布后AI过度使用词汇频率上升，平均变化+15.1%，而基线词仅变化-4.5%。10种语言的长历时分析显示2022年后增幅超过早期小幅波动。效果量小于科学英语。

Conclusion: AI相关的词汇偏好不仅限于英语，可能对全球语言使用施加跨语言的同质化压力。研究经过多种验证（不同种子、模型变体、数据规模、模型系列等）支持结论的稳健性。

Abstract: AI-associated lexical shifts have been documented mainly in Scientific English. We extend this work to 34 languages in the WMT News Crawl corpus, refining a split-halves continuation diagnostic that compares GPT-4.1 continuations with matched human gold-standard text. For each language, we derive ranked AI-overused lemmas using log prevalence ratios. We find substantial cross-lingual semantic convergence: semantically related concepts recur across typologically diverse languages, with 'emphasize'-type verbs appearing in 24 of 34 languages. Embedding-based and manual analyses support this pattern. We also examine diachronic uptake in news writing before and after ChatGPT's release. Tracking each language's top 20 AI-overused items, we find prevalence increases in 26 of 34 languages from 2020-2021 to 2023-2024, with a mean change of +15.1%, whilst matched baseline words show no comparable increase (-4.5%). In 10 languages with longer historical coverage, longitudinal analyses show post-2022 increases that exceed the modest shifts observed in earlier periods, though with smaller effect sizes than in Scientific English. We validate our approach extensively, including across seeds, model variants, data sizes, model families, and more. Our findings are consistent with the view that AI-associated lexical preferences extend beyond English and may exert cross-lingual homogenising pressure on global language use.

</details>


### [161] [Learning to Route Languages for Multilingual Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25360)
*Geyang Guo,Hiromi Wakaki,Yuki Mitsufuji,Alan Ritter,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LRPO是一种在线策略优化框架，将语言视为可选变量，通过多语言 rollout 和可训练的语言路由器（多臂老虎机）来平衡语言探索与利用，提升多语言大模型的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略优化方法常隐式地将每个训练问题限制为单一响应语言，或依赖固定主导语言进行监督，未能有效利用多语言语料库中的异构信息。

Method: 提出语言路由策略优化（LRPO），包含：(1) 对每个训练问题生成多语言 rollout，并基于相对质量进行偏好策略更新；(2) 引入可训练的语言路由器（多臂老虎机），自适应平衡对未充分开发语言的探索与对信息量更丰富语言的利用。

Result: 大量实验表明，LRPO持续提升了多语言性能，证明了自适应语言路由能够有效利用跨语言知识进行训练。

Conclusion: 自适应语言路由策略能够有效挖掘跨语言知识，为多语言大模型训练提供更丰富的信号，资源已开源。

Abstract: Large language models~(LLMs) are trained on heterogeneous multilingual corpora, yet existing policy optimization methods often implicitly restrict each training question to a single response language or rely on a fixed dominant language for supervision. We propose language-routed policy optimization (LRPO), an online policy optimization framework that treats language as a selectable variable. LRPO elicits multilingual rollouts for each training question and integrates their relative quality into preference-based policy updates, increasing the diversity and informativeness of training signals under the fixed rollout budget. To adaptively determine which languages to explore during reinforcement learning, we introduce a trainable language router formulated as a multi-armed bandit, balancing exploration of underutilized languages with exploitation of more informative ones. Extensive experiments show that LRPO consistently improves multilingual performance, demonstrating that adaptive language routing enables effective cross-lingual knowledge exploitation for training. We release all the resources at https://github.com/Guochry/LRPO.

</details>


### [162] [AuthTrace: Diagnosing Evidence Construction in Thematically Dense Single-Author Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25382)
*Xiaoqing Wu,Feifei Li,Haoliang Ming,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AuthTrace，首个将多种证据构建范式（块检索、智能体记忆、知识图谱遍历、主题索引）统一在单一语料库和查询集上的诊断基准，利用单作者文集的双重性质提供2,099个带有完整黄金证据和扇入梯度的实例。实验发现：证据召回比精度更能预测答案质量；扇入暴露了范式特定的崩溃模式；全上下文提示在所有系统中均失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据构建系统（块检索、智能体记忆、知识图谱遍历、主题索引）在各自独立的基准上评估，语料库和指标不兼容，导致跨范式诊断无法进行。

Method: 提出AuthTrace基准，利用单作者文集（风格、主题、词汇一致的主题密集语料）构建统一评估平台，提供2,099个实例及完整黄金证据，以扇入梯度作为主要诊断轴，比较八种系统在两种QA模型上的表现。

Result: (1) 证据召回（而非精度）是答案质量的主导预测因子（r=0.96）；(2) 扇入暴露了范式特定的崩溃模式，平面检索退化的速度是结构化证据系统的3倍；(3) 全上下文提示在所有系统中一致失败。

Conclusion: 证据构建是超越原始语料暴露的必要能力，不同范式在扇入条件下的退化模式存在显著差异。

Abstract: Evidence construction systems--chunk retrieval, agent memory, knowledge-graph traversal, and thematic indexing--are evaluated on separate benchmarks with incompatible corpora and metrics, making cross-paradigm diagnosis impossible. We introduce AuthTrace, the first diagnostic benchmark that places all major paradigms on a single corpus and query set by exploiting the dual nature of single-author collections. Built on thematically dense corpora where all texts share style, topic, and vocabulary, AuthTrace provides 2,099 instances with exhaustive gold evidence and a fan-in gradient as the primary diagnostic axis. Comparing eight systems across two QA models, we find that (1) evidence recall--not precision--is the dominant predictor of answer quality (r = 0.96); (2) fan-in exposes paradigm-specific collapse patterns, with flat retrieval degrading 3x faster than structured-evidence systems; and (3) full-context prompting fails uniformly, establishing evidence construction as a necessary capacity beyond raw corpus exposure.

</details>


### [163] [GeoMathCode: Understanding Interleaved Math-Code Reasoning for Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25384)
*Yingji Zhang,Yong Dai,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GeoMathCode框架，利用程序化表示作为中间视觉输出，通过解耦推理与代码生成步骤提升几何问题求解能力。实验表明，监督微调使推理流形更结构化，层级化代码结构在潜空间中形成解耦子空间，且比视觉表示包含更多数学符号信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在几何问题中表现优异，但现有方法缺乏可解释的中间推理步骤。为了更好模拟人类解题过程中辅助视觉构建（如辅助线、点）的认知机制，需要引入程序化中间表示来增强几何解释能力和教育清晰度。

Method: 提出GeoMathCode框架，将程序化表示作为中间视觉输出；在潜空间中解耦推理与代码生成步骤；通过监督微调优化推理流形结构；分析层级化代码语法结构在潜空间中的解耦特性。

Result: 推理与代码生成步骤可在潜空间中解耦；监督微调使推理流形更结构化和信息丰富；层级化代码语法结构形成解耦的潜子空间；代码表示比视觉表示包含更多数学符号信息。

Conclusion: GeoMathCode通过程序化中间表示有效提升了几何推理的可解释性和性能，代码结构在潜空间中的解耦特性为数学推理提供了更丰富的符号信息，优于传统视觉表示方法。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning is a hallmark of human intelligence, requiring logical deduction, symbolic manipulation, and abstract thinking. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on geometry problems through multi-step reasoning. To better emulate human problem-solving, intermediate steps can incorporate auxiliary visual constructions, such as additional lines or points, which improve geometric interpretation and educational clarity. In this work, we introduce the GeoMathCode, where programmatic representations serve as intermediate visual outputs. We further conduct an in-depth analysis of the underlying reasoning geometry. Experimental results show that reasoning and code generation steps can be disentangled in the latent space, while supervised fine-tuning (SFT) makes the reasoning manifold more structured and informative. Moreover, hierarchical syntactic code structures emerge as disentangled latent subspaces, and contain more mathematical symbolic information than visual representations.

</details>


### [164] [Proactive for Uncertainty: Cause-Aware Error Diagnosis and Interactive Clarification for Spoken Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25404)
*Yizhou Peng,Ziyang Ma,Changsong Liu,Yi-Wen Chao,Xie Chen,Eng Siong Chng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果感知错误恢复范式，通过一组轻量级检测器利用ASR深层潜在表示，将词级错误细分为感知、理解和删除三类失败，使LLM能够执行有针对性的多轮澄清策略，在多种口音、失真和领域下实现WER降低30%、下游任务提升17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联ASR-LLM流水线在工业口语对话系统中虽被广泛使用（因其解耦设计保证了感知可验证性），但存在错误传播问题：转录失败会级联到后续组件，降低交互质量。传统的ASR置信度过滤方法无法检测删除错误，也无法区分声学（听不清）和语言（不理解）两类不匹配，而这两类错误需要不同的恢复策略。

Method: 提出因果感知错误恢复范式：(1) 设计一组轻量级、高精度的检测器，利用ASR深层潜在表示将词级错误解耦为感知失败（听不清）、理解失败（不理解）和删除失败三类；(2) 将细粒度诊断信息输入LLM，使其能够编排有针对性的多轮澄清策略，将模糊信号转化为流畅交互。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 在领域迁移错误上的召回率相比基线提升一倍以上（57.96% vs. 23.66%）；(2) 在不同口音、失真和领域场景下，WER降低高达30%；(3) 下游任务性能提升17%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过细粒度的错误归因诊断，突破了传统置信度过滤的局限性，为级联ASR-LLM系统在工业口语对话系统中的鲁棒性提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Cascaded Automatic Speech Recognition -- Large Language Model (ASR-LLM) pipelines remain popular for industrial Spoken Dialogue Systems (SDS), primarily because their decoupled design ensures perceptual verifiability. However, cascaded systems suffer from error propagation, as transcription failures inevitably cascade to subsequent components, thereby degrading the final interaction quality. Although ASR confidence scores offer a simple filter for unreliable inputs, this approach is fundamentally limited because it typically fails to detect deletion errors or to distinguish between acoustic (inability to hear clearly) and linguistic (inability to understand) mismatches, both of which require targeted recovery strategies. In this paper, we propose a cause-aware error recovery paradigm that fundamentally rethinks robustness in SDS. Unlike traditional confidence filtering, we introduce a suite of small precision-focused detectors that exploit deep ASR latent representations to disentangle token-level errors into perception, comprehension, and deletion failures. This fine-grained diagnostic intelligence empowers the LLM to orchestrate targeted, multi-turn clarification strategies, effectively transforming ambiguous signals into seamless user interactions. Experimental results validate the precision of our approach, which more than doubles the recall on domain-shift errors (57.96% vs. 23.66%) compared to baselines. Crucially, this diagnostic precision yields up to a 30% reduction in WER and a 17% improvement on the downstream task across diverse accents, distortions, and domains.

</details>


### [165] [LLM-as-a-Reviewer: Benchmarking Their Ability, Divergence, and Prompt Injection Resistance as Paper Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25415)
*Lingyao Li,Junjie Xiong,Changjia Zhu,Runlong Yu,Chen Chen,Junyu Wang,Renkai Ma,Zhicong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在学术同行评审中存在系统性高估弱论文、与人类评审在主题权重上存在分歧、易受隐形字体攻击等漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在同行评审中被日益使用，但其可靠性、与人类判断的对齐程度以及对对抗攻击的鲁棒性仍不明确，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 在NeurIPS和ICLR的898篇论文上构建基准，评估12个LLM在评分校准、与人类评审的分歧、以及通过隐形字体攻击植入的提示注入抵抗能力三个维度上的表现。

Result: LLMs系统性高估弱论文；与人类在主题权重上存在分歧（低估Clarity、高估Reproducibility）；生成更长但词汇多样性更低的评审；隐形字体攻击的提示注入高度有效，可使低分论文被提升至接收级别。

Conclusion: LLMs在结构化评估中有一定价值，但需建立针对内在偏见和对抗性风险的安全保障才能整合到同行评审中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in academic peer review, yet their reliability, alignment with human judgment, and robustness to adversarial attacks remain poorly understood. We present a systematic benchmark of LLM-as-a-Reviewer on 898 papers stratified from NeurIPS and ICLR, evaluating 12 LLMs along three axes: rating calibration, divergence from human reviewers, and resistance to prompt injection embedded via an invisible font-mapping attack. We find that LLMs systematically overrate weaker submissions and diverge from humans in topical emphasis, under-flagging Clarity and over-flagging Reproducibility, while producing reviews two to three times longer with lower lexical diversity and a more standardized vocabulary. Prompt injection remains highly effective. Simple hidden instructions can promote low-scoring papers to acceptance-level ratings in a substantial fraction of cases, with effectiveness varying sharply across model families. While LLMs offer utility in structuring evaluations, their integration into peer review requires safeguards against both intrinsic biases and adversarial risks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [In Search of the Ingredients of Open-Endedness: Replicating Picbreeder with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23908)
*Sam Earle,Kay Arulkumaran,Andrew Dai,Akarsh Kumar,Julian Togelius,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究用前沿视觉语言模型(VLM)替代人类用户，复现了经典开放式演化系统Picbreeder，并对比分析了AI与人类在开放式搜索中的表现差异，探讨了探索噪声、行为多样性和叙事记忆等机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人工智能体是否具备像人类一样进行富有成果的、无引导的开放式发现能力，即能否在无人干预的情况下持续生成新颖且有意义的产物。

Method: 以Picbreeder为基准，用前沿视觉语言模型(VLM)替代人类用户进行交互式演化，通过系统发育复杂度、视觉与语义显著性及新颖性等指标量化AI与人类输出的差异，并引入探索噪声、行为多样性和叙事记忆等干预变量分析因果因素。

Result: AI系统与人类基线之间存在明显的定性差异；添加探索噪声、行为多样性和叙事记忆对系统输出特征有不同程度的影响。

Conclusion: 当前VLM驱动的AI在开放式搜索中的表现与人类存在本质差异，需进一步探索机制性改进以缩小这一差距。

Abstract: We are in the midst of large-scale industrial and academic efforts to automate the processes of scientific, technological and creative production through AI-driven assistants. Historically, a fundamental property of these processes in their human form has been their open-endedness: their capacity for generating a seemingly endless supply of novel and meaningful new forms. Do artificial agents have any capacity for such fruitful unguided discovery? To answer this question, we turn to Picbreeder, the canonical exemplar of human-driven open-ended search, in which users collaboratively generated a diverse library of images through interactive evolution of small neural networks. We replicate Picbreeder, replacing human users with frontier Vision Language Models (VLMs). We observe clear qualitative differences between the output of our system and the historical human baseline, and attempt to characterize them using metrics of phylogenetic complexity and visual and semantic salience and novelty. In an effort to identify some of the causal factors contributing these differences, we study the addition of exploratory noise to the agents' selection process, of behavioral diversity between agents, and of narrative momentum in the form of memory of past actions. We make our code available at https://github.com/smearle/picbreeder-vlm.

</details>


### [167] [Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
*Noam Michael,Daniel BenShushan,Jacob Bien,Don A. Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在不同任务中的置信度校准问题。研究发现，当前的LLMs像人类一样，平均而言过于自信（自信超过准确率）。但这种倾向受到强烈的难易效应调节：在困难任务上过度自信最明显，而在简单任务上则表现出显著的不自信。研究者还开发了LifeEval测试，用于评估模型在不同难度水平上的校准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在不同难度任务上的置信度校准情况，特别是验证LLMs是否和人类一样存在过度自信偏差，以及难易效应如何影响这种校准。

Method: 通过预先注册的对照研究，系统性地评估LLMs在不同任务上的置信度与准确率之间的差异。开发了LifeEval测试集，用于评估模型在不同难度水平上的校准表现。

Result: 当前LLMs平均表现出过度自信（置信度超过准确率）；存在显著的难易效应：困难任务上过度最严重，简单任务上反而出现明显的不自信。

Conclusion: LLMs的置信度校准受任务难度调节，与人类类似存在难易效应；LifeEval可用于评估模型在不同难度上的校准能力。

Abstract: We investigate the calibration of large language models' (LLMs') confidence across diverse tasks. The results of our preregistered study show that the current crop of LLMs are, like people, too sure they are right: confidence exceeds accuracy, on average. Importantly, however, this tendency is moderated by a powerful hard-easy effect, wherein overconfidence is greatest on difficult tests; by contrast, easy tests actually show substantial underconfidence. We develop LifeEval, a test for evaluating model calibration across levels of difficulty.

</details>


### [168] [How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xinkai You,Wenjing Yan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化了推理大模型中的"思维冗余"现象，发现61%-93%的推理步骤是冗余的，并从理论上证明这种冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性必然结果，而非模型特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理模型通过长思维链解决问题，但大量推理步骤是否必要从未被大规模量化或从第一性原理解释。

Method: 形式化定义了推理冗余度ρ：在正确推理轨迹中，可被截断的最大尾部步数比例，使得模型被迫终止思考后仍能输出正确答案。在四个前沿推理模型和两个数学基准上进行了大规模量化，并通过理论证明推导冗余的结构性成因。

Result: 在8个(模型, 基准)组合中，步级冗余度高达61%-93%；6个组合的中位关键前缀仅为一个分段步；冗余度随问题难度增加而下降，但即使在最难的Level-5问题上冗余度仍有46%-85%。理论证明：在长度无关的结果奖励下，不存在有限最优期望停止时间。

Conclusion: 过度思考不是个别模型的bug，而是当前推理模型训练方式（使用长度无关的结果奖励）的结构性属性，无论使用何种RL算法、基础模型或数据分布。

Abstract: Reasoning-capable large language models solve hard problems by emitting long chains of thought, paying heavily in latency, GPU time, and energy. Casual inspection of their traces reveals extensive reformulation, verification, and circular self-reflection, yet how much of this deliberation is actually necessary has never been measured at scale or explained from first principles. This paper closes both gaps.
  We formalise reasoning redundancy directly in terms of the reasoning model itself: the redundancy of a correct trace is the largest fraction of its trailing segmented steps that can be truncated while $π$, forced to terminate thinking and emit a final answer, still produces the correct answer. A large-scale quantification across four frontier reasoning models and two mathematical benchmarks shows that step-level redundancy is consistently high -- between 61% and 93% across the 8 (model, benchmark) conditions we study, with the median critical prefix equal to a single segmented step in six of the eight conditions -- that the finding is robust to the choice of judge family, and that although $ρ$ decreases with problem difficulty on MATH-500, all four models remain substantially redundant ($ρ\in [46\%, 85\%]$) even on the hardest Level-5 problems.
  We then prove that this redundancy is a structural consequence of length-agnostic outcome rewards, not a model-specific artefact: under any such reward, no finite expected stopping time is optimal. The result holds regardless of RL algorithm, base model, data distribution, or whether the policy is obtained via RL or distillation; over-thinking is therefore not a bug to be patched in individual models but a structural property of how current reasoning models are trained. Code: https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough

</details>


### [169] [Quantum Frog: Emergent Cooperation and Difficulty Scaling in a Quantized-Time Cooperative Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23930)
*Saad Mankarious*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍《Quantum Frog》——一款基于“量子化时间”机制的双人合作游戏，并利用强化学习（RL）分析游戏难度、最优策略、合作差距及涌现的合作策略。结果表明：量子化时间机制下“冲刺策略”最优；不协调的双人游戏难度远高于单玩家面对六倍车流；合作训练可将成功率提升32-34个百分点，并把步数从~90降至~6；涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺而非复杂位置协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一款基于“量子化时间”（环境仅在玩家行动时推进）机制的双人合作游戏《Quantum Frog》，并通过强化学习系统性地回答四个设计问题：难度随车流密度如何变化、最优单智能体策略是什么、独立双智能体与协作双智能体之间的合作差距有多大、以及激励合作时涌现的联合策略是什么。

Method: 构建8×8网格的《Quantum Frog》游戏，采用五种渐进式RL训练方法：Tabular Q-Learning、Deep Q-Network (DQN)、Independent DQN (IDQN) 以及带有集中式critic的Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimisation (MAPPO)。评估车流密度为1至6辆车时的训练效果，分析单智能体和双智能体场景下的表现差异。

Result: (i) 量子化时间机制使“冲刺策略”（每步直行向上）成为全局最优，因最小化了暴露于车流的时间；(ii) 加入不协调的第二玩家比单专家玩家面对六倍车流更困难；(iii) 合作训练相比独立智能体，联合成功率提升32-34个百分点，回合步数从~90降至~6；(iv) 涌现的合作策略是同步冲刺，而非复杂的位置协调策略。

Conclusion: 量子化时间机制从根本上改变了最优策略，使简单冲刺优于复杂规避；共享激励足以使智能体在时间关键型合作任务中自发对齐行为，无需显式协调机制；研究成果为《Quantum Frog》的商业设计提供了经验性指导，并为环境机制如何塑造多智能体学习动态提供了更广泛的见解。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Quantum Frog}, a two-player cooperative game built on a novel \emph{quantized-time} mechanic in which the environment advances only when a player acts. Inspired by the classic arcade game Frogger, Quantum Frog requires two frogs to cross an 8$\times$8 grid of traffic and reach the far side together. We use reinforcement learning (RL) as an analytical lens to answer four design questions: (1) how does game difficulty scale with traffic density, (2) what is the optimal single-agent policy and why, (3) how large is the cooperation gap between independent and cooperative two-agent play, and (4) what joint strategy emerges when agents are incentivised to cooperate? We train agents through five escalating stages, Tabular Q-Learning, Deep Q-Network (\DQN), Independent \DQN~(\IDQN), and Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimisation (\MAPPO\ with a centralised critic), evaluating each against traffic densities of one to six cars. Our key findings are: (i) the quantized-time mechanic makes a \emph{rush strategy} (moving directly upward at every step) universally optimal, as time exposure to traffic is minimised; (ii) adding an uncoordinated second player is harder than sextupling the traffic for a single expert player; (iii) cooperative training recovers +32--34 percentage points of joint success rate relative to independent agents and reduces episode length from $\sim$90 to $\sim$6 steps; and (iv) the emergent cooperative strategy is synchronised rushing, not complex positional coordination, illustrating that shared incentives alone suffice to align agents in time-critical cooperative tasks. These findings provide concrete, empirically grounded guidance for the commercial design of Quantum Frog and offer broader insights into the role of environment mechanics in shaping multi-agent learning dynamics.

</details>


### [170] [BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
*Zhiming Chang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BODHI方法，通过结构化C到Python翻译指南增强大模型在操作系统内核形式化规约生成中的表现，在OSV-Bench基准上将最优Pass@1从55.10%提升至96.73%，覆盖9种模型并取得+11%~+32%的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统内核形式化验证需要精确的系统调用规约，人工编写规约需要深厚的领域知识且成本高昂，因此需要使用大语言模型自动化这一过程。然而现有方法在OSV-Bench基准上Pass@1仅为55.10%，性能不足。

Method: 提出BODHI（领域知识提示方法），在标准少样本提示基础上，增加一个涵盖15类领域特定翻译模式的结构化C到Python翻译指南。该指南受结构化思维链（SCoT）启发，按关注点分离原则组织翻译，将前置条件提取和后置条件生成作为不同类别处理。

Result: 在6个提供商的9种模型（覆盖密集、混合专家和推理架构）上评估，BODHI对所有模型均有提升，增益范围为+11%至+32%。最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI）达到96.73% Pass@1。BODHI同时减少了语法错误和语义错误，对具有足够指令跟随能力的模型效果最强。

Conclusion: 领域知识注入是一种模型无关的技术，能够显著弥合通用代码生成与形式化规约合成之间的差距。

Abstract: The formal verification of operating system kernels requires precise specifications that capture the intended behavior of system calls. Writing these specifications manually demands deep domain expertise, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate the process. However, in OSV-Bench, a benchmark of 245 specification generation tasks derived from the Hyperkernel OS kernel, the best reported Pass@1 is 55.10%. We propose a domain knowledge prompting method (BODHI), which augments the standard few-shot prompt with a structured C-to-Python translation guide covering 15 categories of domain-specific translation patterns. Inspired by Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting, the guide organizes translation by separation of concerns, addressing pre-condition extraction and post-condition generation as distinct categories. Evaluated on nine models from six providers (Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba), covering dense, mixture-of-experts and reasoning architectures, BODHI improves every model tested, with gains ranging from +11% to +32%. The best configuration (Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI) reaches 96.73% Pass@1. BODHI reduces both syntax and semantic errors, with the strongest effect on models that have sufficient instruction-following capability to utilize structured reference material. These results demonstrate that domain knowledge injection is a model-agnostic technique that substantially bridges the gap between general-purpose code generation and formal specification synthesis.

</details>


### [171] [When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
*Boyu Xiao,Xiuqi Tian,Xuwen Song,Haochun Wang,Guanchun Song,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Med-Stress压力测试框架，评估LLM在临床对话中面对逐步升级压力时的信念稳定性。发现前沿LLM存在医学知识与鲁棒性之间的分离，并提出两种缓解方法：推理阶段的RBED防御和训练阶段的R-FT微调，后者几乎完全消除信念变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在医学基准测试中表现优异，但在多轮临床对话中会出现严重的"谄媚行为"（sycophancy），即在不断升级的压力下放弃最初正确的诊断。现有评估忽视了这种信念稳定性的缺失，因此需要专门的压力测试框架来系统评估这一缺陷。

Method: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架，通过逐步升级的临床对话压力来评估LLM的信念稳定性。针对发现的鲁棒性问题，提出两种解决方案：(1) RBED（基于角色的认知防御），一种轻量级推理时防御方法；(2) R-FT（韧性导向微调），一种训练时方法，通过内化基于证据的抗压能力来提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在9个前沿LLM上的实验发现，高初始诊断能力并不等同于高信念稳定性，导致多个模型存在显著的知识-鲁棒性差距。R-FT方法几乎完全消除了信念变化，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 医学知识与鲁棒性之间存在可分离性，单纯的基准测试准确率不能反映模型在临床对话中的实际可靠性。通过针对性的训练策略可以有效缓解LLM在面对压力时的信念动摇问题，提升其在临床场景中的可信度。

Abstract: Despite strong medical benchmark accuracy, LLMs can exhibit severe multi-turn sycophancy in clinical dialogue, abandoning initial correct diagnosis under escalating pressure. We propose \textbf{\textsc{Med-Stress}}, a targeted stress test framework that evaluates belief stability under escalating pressure. Across nine frontier large language models (LLMs), we find a clear dissociation between medical knowledge and robustness: high initial diagnostic capability does not imply high belief stability, yielding large knowledge-robustness gaps for several LLMs. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a lightweight inference-time defense, \textbf{\texttt{RBED}} (\textbf{R}ole-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{E}pistemic \textbf{D}efense), and \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} (\textbf{R}esilience-oriented \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning), a training-time approach that internalizes evidence-based resistance to pressure. Experiments show that \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} nearly eliminates belief change and substantially improves robustness.

</details>


### [172] [Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
*Wang Rui,Lu Diannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机(CIM)与基于LLM的智能体系统集成，利用LangGraph和LangChain框架实现QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重迭代和文献方案快速验证等任务，并基于全国产大模型和CIM硬件实现实用赋能，同时发现智能体辅助量子计算的迭代知识可反向增强智能体自身问题解决能力的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算设备在解决NP完全问题上具有强大潜力，但其建模复杂性对非专家构成显著障碍，且约束权重和建模方法的繁琐迭代也消耗专家大量精力，因此需要一种自动化智能系统来降低使用门槛并提升效率。

Method: 将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机(CIM)与基于大语言模型的智能体系统集成，采用LangGraph和LangChain框架实现模型校准、权重迭代和方案验证等任务的自动化执行。

Result: 大语言模型能够有效执行QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献报道方案的快速验证；所有任务可基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件完全实现，达成全国产智能体大模型与硬件的实用赋能。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了稳健的技术集成，为后续研究奠定基础；同时识别了大模型和量子计算两个前沿领域当前阶段的持续挑战，并意外发现了一种新范式：智能体辅助量子计算迭代中积累的知识可反向增强智能体自身的问题解决能力。

Abstract: Quantum computing devices are recognized as powerful tools for solving NP-complete problems. However, the intricacy of their modeling presents notable barriers for non-specialists, while the tedious iteration of constraint weights and modeling methodologies also consumes substantial effort on the part of experts. To address these challenges, this study integrates a femtosecond laser-pumped Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) with an LLM-driven agentic system by leveraging the LangGraph and LangChain frameworks. Comprehensive investigations demonstrate that large language models (LLMs) can effectively perform such tasks in modeling as QUBO/Ising model calibration, constraint weight decision iteration and rapid validation of literature-reported schemes. Notably, all these tasks can be fully implemented based on domestic large models, combined with domestically developed CIM hardware, we truly achieve the practical empowerment of quantum CIM that fully relies on all-domestic agentic large models and hardware. This work successfully realizes robust technological integration, laying a solid foundation for subsequent research. Nevertheless, it also identifies the persisting challenges in the two cutting-edge fields of large models and quantum computing at the current stage. Encouragingly, we unexpectedly discover a promising new paradigm where accumulated knowledge from agent-assisted quantum computing iterations reciprocally enhances the agent's own problem-solving capability, thereby addressing these challenges.

</details>


### [173] [Operationalizing Reconstructive Authority: Runtime Construction, Dependency Resolution, and Execution Gating in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23935)
*Marcelo Fernandez - TraslaIA*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运行时执行模型，将权威可重构性作为动作执行的条件，引入“暂停”状态处理不确定观测，并设计了恢复循环机制，保证安全性与条件活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体系统不仅因错误决策而失败，也因执行一个在运行时其权限已不再有效的决策而失败。现有工作定义了重构权威（RAM）作为有效执行的条件，但缺乏运行时执行机制。

Method: 提出扩展的运行时执行模型，包含三种状态（允许/拒绝/暂停），并设计了具体的执行协议，包括动态依赖解析、权威重构、显式决策语义，以及结合IML漂移检测和ACP执行控制的恢复循环。

Result: 该模型保证了安全性（无动作在无构建权威时执行）和条件活性（当权威定义变量可观测时恢复执行）。

Conclusion: 本文将重构权威操作化为运行时执行机制，提供了在真实系统中应用RAM所需的执行语义。

Abstract: Autonomous agent systems fail not only due to incorrect decisions, but due to executing decisions whose authority no longer holds at runtime. Prior work defined Reconstructive Authority (RAM) as a condition for valid execution: actions are permitted only if authority can be constructed from current state.
  This paper addresses enforcement at runtime: how to enforce this condition in a running system.
  We introduce a runtime execution model in which authority is evaluated at action time and execution is conditioned on its constructibility. This extends the execution state space beyond admit/deny with a third state, halt, representing cases where authority is undefined due to incomplete or uncertain observability.
  We define a concrete execution protocol including dynamic dependency resolution, authority reconstruction, and explicit decision semantics. We further introduce a Recovery Loop that integrates drift detection (IML) with execution control (ACP), allowing the system to suspend execution, acquire missing information, and re-attempt authority reconstruction.
  We show that this model guarantees safety -- no action is executed without constructible authority -- and conditional liveness: execution resumes when authority-defining variables become observable.
  This work operationalizes reconstructive authority as a runtime enforcement mechanism, providing the execution semantics required to apply RAM in real systems.

</details>


### [174] [BoxLitE: A Faithful Knowledge Base Embedding Based on Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23937)
*Bruno F. Lourenço,Hesham Morgan,Ana Ozaki,Aleksandar Pavlović,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoxLitE，一种面向DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$的知识库嵌入模型，通过将概念映射到凸区域并结合凸优化实现弱忠实的嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识图谱嵌入方法在利用凸性能力进行实际学习任务方面存在不足，且缺乏对DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$这类描述逻辑本体知识（TBox）的有效支持。

Method: 提出BoxLitE模型，将概念映射为向量空间中的凸区域（box区域），支持DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$的推理规则；将嵌入学习任务形式化为凸优化问题，确保得到满足弱忠实性条件的嵌入。

Result: 理论上证明了对于任意可满足的DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库，都存在满足弱忠实性的BoxLitE嵌入；实验上展示了如何通过凸优化获取具有理想忠实性属性的嵌入。

Conclusion: BoxLitE通过凸区域表示与凸优化相结合，为描述逻辑知识库的嵌入学习提供了一种理论上可保证忠实性的有效方案。

Abstract: Knowledge base (KB) embeddings aim at combining the capability of classical knowledge graph embeddings to generalize the information present in facts, the ABox, with conceptual knowledge represented in an ontology language, the TBox. Several authors have recently explored the idea of mapping concepts to convex regions in a vector space. This is useful to represent hierarchies, typically present in TBoxes, since more general concepts can be mapped to larger regions, containing those regions associated with more specific concepts. However, the power of convexity is rarely leveraged during the actual learning tasks. Here, we introduce BoxLitE, a KB embedding model for DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ that allows for convex optimization. We show that for any satisfiable DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ KB, there is a BoxLitE embedding that is a weakly faithful model. As a proof of concept, we show how to formulate the KB embedding task as a convex optimization problem and how to obtain embeddings with such desirable faithfulness properties.

</details>


### [175] [Authority Inversion in LLM-Mediated Ubiquitous Systems: When Models Trust Users Over Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23938)
*Long Zhang,Zi-bo Qin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现大语言模型在多模态融合中存在"权威倒置"现象：数值传感器数据因格式劣势被自然语言用户主张主导，导致模型决策严重偏向语言信息。提出几何框架、审计指标（CIR/AAI）及推理层干预方法GAC，实验证明该问题普遍且严重，但可通过理论指导的因果干预有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多模态融合研究忽略了当传感器测量值与用户主张冲突时的隐性权威分配问题，物理传感数据本应具有更高优先级，但LLM将权威分配隐式地编码在习得表示中，且可能受到输入格式的严重扭曲，威胁系统可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出上下文集成的几何框架；2) 定义两个可计算审计指标：上下文集成比（CIR）和权威对齐指数（AAI）；3) 提出几何权威校准（GAC），一种推理时层级别干预方法，通过理论指导的因果注入抑制错位的用户权威。

Result: 在4个模型（4B-35B参数、3种架构）、4个数据集、576个冲突实例上，数值任务中模型对传感器的信任接近零（AAI=-0.805, Cohen's d=-2.14），且不受模型容量影响。理论指导的因果注入可翻转80.2%的错误决策（随机控制<0.4%）。GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%。

Conclusion: LLM系统中的权威分配必须被明确审计并根据应用场景具体配置，而非隐式依赖模型内部表示。该工作揭示了多模态融合中一个此前未被关注但至关重要的问题，并提供了可操作的诊断与缓解方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly fuse heterogeneous inputs in ubiquitous systems. Yet, how LLMs implicitly allocate authority when sensor measurements and user claims conflict remains unexamined, raising critical reliability concerns for deployments where physical sensing must retain priority. Unlike explicit traditional fusion, LLMs bury authority allocation within learned representations. We discover this allocation is severely format-dependent: numerical sensor data fails to integrate into answer-relevant model directions, allowing natural-language claims to dominate the final decision, a phenomenon we term \textbf{Authority Inversion}.To diagnose and mitigate this, we develop a geometric framework of context integration, introduce two computable audit metrics, specifically the Context Integration Ratio (CIR) and Authority Alignment Index (AAI), and propose Geometric Authority Calibration (GAC), an inference-time layer-level intervention to suppress misplaced user authority. Evaluating four models (4B to 35B parameters, three architectures) across four datasets totaling 576 conflict instances reveals extreme inversion: on numerical tasks, models exhibit near-zero sensor trust (AAI = -0.805, Cohen's d = -2.14), unaffected by model capacity. Validating our geometric framework, theory-guided causal injection flips 80.2\% of incorrect decisions (vs. <0.4\% for random controls). Practically, GAC improves HAR accuracy from 0 -- 1.6\% to 21.9 -- 27.5\%, outperforming prompting baselines. Ultimately, authority allocation in LLM-mediated systems must be explicitly audited and application-specifically configured rather than left implicit.

</details>


### [176] [DRIVE: Modeling Skills at the Reasoning and Interaction Levels for Web Agents under Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23939)
*Xirui Liu,Sihang Zhou,Yanning Hou,Rong Zhou,Haoyuan Chen,Maolin He,Siwei Wang,Hao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIVE框架，将Web智能体的历史经验解耦为自然语言推理技能（可跨网站迁移的任务逻辑）和程序化交互技能（可执行的具体页面操作），通过场景感知协调机制和技能级反思实现自适应检索与技能库优化，在WebArena五个域上取得52.8%平均成功率，超过无技能基线7.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体方法将推理知识和交互知识统一存储，导致抽象表示在具体页面上缺乏可执行性，而具体表示无法跨域泛化，限制了能力积累。

Method: 提出DRIVE双层级技能建模框架：将历史经验解耦为自然语言推理技能（可迁移的任务逻辑）和程序化交互技能（可执行的操作）。采用场景感知协调机制根据任务语义自适应检索和调用双层级技能，并通过技能级反射识别层次特定故障模式，实现技能库的定向扩展与精炼。

Result: 在WebArena五个域上平均任务成功率达52.8%，超过无技能基线7.3个百分点。消融实验表明推理技能和交互技能提供不同且互补的收益，支持将可迁移任务逻辑与可执行页面级操作分离的有效性。

Conclusion: DRIVE通过显式解耦推理知识与交互知识，有效解决了Web智能体中表示粒度矛盾导致的泛化和可执行性问题，验证了双层级技能分离设计的互补优势。

Abstract: Web agents require both high-level reasoning (for task decomposition) and low-level interactions (for page elements manipulation) to conduct different tasks. However, these knowledge types differ fundamentally: reasoning knowledge (e.g., booking a flight requires first searching for routes) is abstract and transferable across websites, while interaction knowledge (e.g., clicking the Search button at a specific coordinate on Site A) depends heavily on page-specific contexts. Existing methods store experiences uniformly. This creates a dilemma: abstract representations lose executability on concrete pages, while concrete representations fail to generalize across domains. This entanglement limits capability accumulation: on new websites, agents either fail to recognize reusable task logic due to surface-level differences or attempt infeasible actions from outdated page structures. To disentangle them, we propose DRIVE, a dual-level skill modeling framework separating historical experience into natural language reasoning skills, which capture transferable task logic, and programmatic interaction skills, grounding abstract actions to executable operations. A scene-aware coordination mechanism adaptively retrieves and invokes these dual-level skills based on task semantics. DRIVE also uses skill-level reflection to identify hierarchy-specific failure modes, enabling targeted skill library expansion and refinement. Experiments across five WebArena domains show DRIVE attains an average task success rate of 52.8%, exceeding the skill-free baseline by 7.3 percentage points. Further ablations show reasoning and interaction skills provide distinct, complementary benefits, supporting separation of transferable task logic from executable page-level operations.

</details>


### [177] [Residual Drift Dominates Contradiction in Multi-Turn Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23940)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现多轮推理系统的失败模式不是逻辑矛盾，而是"可满足偏移"——系统内部状态一致但返回答案违反先前承诺。作者构建了DRIFT-Bench基准（816个测试问题），评估了四种方法，发现MUS-Repair在所有设置中表现最佳，但修复后残留错误中98-100%是可满足偏移，矛盾几乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设多轮推理系统失败的主要模式是逻辑矛盾，本文旨在揭示实际失败模式，并构建系统性基准来分类和衡量不同类型的推理失败。

Method: 构建DRIFT-Bench基准，包含816个跨三个约束域的测试问题，使用求解器自动标注失败类型。评估四种修复方法（含MUS-Repair）在四个开源模型（8B-120B参数）上的表现。

Result: MUS-Repair在所有设置中表现最佳（比最优非MUS基线高+1.8至+15.0个百分点）。但修复后残留错误中98-100%是可满足偏移，逻辑矛盾降至接近零。

Conclusion: 可靠的多轮系统需要单独验证返回答案是否尊重已维护的状态，因为现有方法无法消除可满足偏移这种失败模式。

Abstract: How do multi-turn reasoning systems fail? The expected answer is logical contradiction, in which the system's maintained state becomes unsatisfiable. We show that the dominant mode is instead satisfiable drift, where the internal state stays consistent while the returned answer silently violates prior commitments. We build DRIFT-Bench (Decomposing Reasoning Into Failure Types), a solver-instrumented benchmark of 816 test problems across three constraint domains, and evaluate four methods on it across four open-weight models (8B-120B parameters). MUS-Repair, which feeds minimal unsatisfiable subsets back to the generator, is strongest in every setting (+1.8 to +15.0 pp over the best non-MUS baseline). But the central finding is what repair leaves behind. After structured feedback, models rarely contradict themselves. They forget. Residual errors are 98-100% satisfiable drift across all settings, while contradiction drops to near zero. Reliable multi-turn systems must separately validate that the returned answer respects the maintained state. Code is available at https://github.com/kaons-research/drift-bench.

</details>


### [178] [MEMOR-E: In-Context and Fine-Tuned LLM Personalization for Alzheimer's Assistive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Eren Sadikoglu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMOR-E，一种集成大语言模型的四足社交辅助机器人，通过药物提醒、认知交互和可解释AI机制，为阿尔茨海默病患者提供个性化的非诊断性认知支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病导致患者记忆和语言能力逐渐衰退，生活独立性降低，亟需社交辅助机器人的支持来改善患者和照护者的生活质量。

Method: 在235名患者音频转录和合成健康对照数据上，微调大语言模型模拟阶段一致的认知行为；利用上下文学习让第二个大语言模型生成领域和严重程度级别的认知错误摘要；集成可解释AI机制实现透明化输出。

Result: MEMOR-E能够生成阶段感知的非诊断性认知摘要，支持个性化辅助交互；可解释AI机制将模型输出转化为透明、可读的证据，实现照护者监督和可信的人机交互。

Conclusion: 通过微调LLM和上下文学习，MEMOR-E在神经心理学语言任务中有效模拟不同阶段认知行为，为阿尔茨海默病患者的个性化机器人辅助提供了可行且可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder marked by progressive declines in memory and language that reduce independence in daily life, motivating socially assistive robotic support. This paper presents MEMOR-E, a mobile quadruped robot with an interactive tablet interface that assists patients and caregivers through medication reminders, routine guidance, memory oriented interactions, and companionship. We evaluated the feasibility of fine tuning large language models (LLMs) to emulate stage consistent cognitive behavior and interpret responses across standard neuropsychological language tasks, using audio transcriptions from 235 Alzheimer's patients and synthetically generated healthy controls. We also report findings on using in context learning (ICL) in LLMs, where a second LLM produced domain and severity level cognitive error summaries. Our results show that MEMOR-E can generate stage aware, non diagnostic cognitive summaries that support personalized assistive interactions, while explainable AI mechanisms translate model outputs into transparent, human readable evidence to enable caregiver oversight and trustworthy human robot interaction.

</details>


### [179] [A Dynamical Framework for Cognitive Processes Based on Transformations and Semantic Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23942)
*Carlo Cattani,Dioneia Motta Monte-Serrat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于控制论视角的认知过程结构与动力学框架，将认知状态表示为状态空间中的元素，通过迭代更新规则演化，并利用范畴论形式化捕捉组合结构，通过不动点论证和收缩条件分析稳定性。计算示例和具体语言应用展示了该框架的操作性特征，将认知建模为趋向不变解释的反馈驱动过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型在统一表示认知过程的动态性、组合结构和稳定性方面存在不足，缺乏一个能够整合动力系统、范畴论和认知建模的通用框架。

Method: 提出状态空间演化模型 X_{t+1} = π(F(f(X_t)))，其中f为内部变换、F为解释映射、π强制语义等价；采用范畴论形式化组合结构；通过不动点论证和收缩条件分析动力学稳定性；辅以计算示例和语言应用验证。

Result: 构建了一个连接动力系统、范畴论和认知建模的统一框架；计算示例展示了框架的操作可行性；语言应用表明上下文相关解释可建模为趋向稳定语义类别的轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架将认知表征为反馈驱动的趋向不变解释的演化过程，为认知建模提供了统一的数学基础，可广泛应用于理解认知的动态稳定机制。

Abstract: This paper proposes a structural and dynamical framework for modeling cognitive processes within a cybernetic perspective. Cognitive states are represented as elements of a state space evolving through an iterative update rule of the form
  \[
  X_{t+1} = π\big(F(f(X_t))\big),
  \]
  where $f$ describes internal transformations, $F$ represents interpretative mappings, and $π$ enforces semantic equivalence.
  The model is interpreted as a feedback system integrating transformation, observation, and stabilization. A categorical formulation is introduced to capture compositional structure, while the associated dynamics are analyzed through fixed-point arguments and contraction conditions ensuring stability.
  To demonstrate the operational character of the framework, a computational illustration is provided, together with a qualitative analysis of the induced dynamics. A concrete linguistic application shows how context-dependent interpretation can be modeled as a trajectory toward a stable semantic class.
  The proposed approach connects dynamical systems, category theory, and cognitive modeling, and provides a unified representation of cognition as a feedback-driven process evolving toward invariant interpretations.

</details>


### [180] [Spacetime Formation under Requirements: Contextual Realization and Form-Dependent Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23943)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出量子概率并非基础性概率框架，而是**语境化时空形成**在固定时空投影下的表现，源于有限状态需求（如有限表征容量、单状态语义稳定性、语境敏感干预等）无法在全局布尔事件结构内实现，从而产生非对易性、干涉项和类量子概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子认知研究通常用量子概率替代经典概率来解释序效应、语境性和全概率律违反，但默认事件结构固定不变。本文试图提出一种不同解释：量子概率是语境化时空形成在固定时空投影下的结果，根源在于有限状态需求与全局布尔事件结构之间的不匹配。

Method: 从需求（有限表征容量、单状态语义稳定性、语境敏感干预、避免显式语境标签、融贯世界形成、主体间可转换性）出发，当这些需求无法在单一全局布尔事件结构中实现时，在固定时空投影下表现为非对易性、干涉和类量子概率。借鉴先前单状态语境性方法，将经典语境记账成本重新解释为语境化时空形成的固定时空影子。

Result: 经典表示中的辅助记忆或语境标签对应局部布尔逻辑世界之间的类联络不匹配；干涉项是局部经典实现贡献经非平凡粘合后投影回固定经典时空形式时产生的交叉项。最终形成一个**先验-操作实在论**框架：对象性、事件性、概率性和时空都被视为需求下的实现形式，客观性由跨观察者和历史依赖的时空形成中不变的不变量定义。

Conclusion: 量子概率不是基础性的概率框架，而是更深层**语境化时空形成**机制在固定时空投影下的表面效应，为量子认知中的异象提供了一种新的实在论解释路径。

Abstract: Quantum cognition often explains order effects, contextuality, and violations of the law of total probability by replacing classical probability with quantum probability on a fixed event structure. This paper proposes a different interpretation: quantum probability is the fixed-spacetime projection of contextual spacetime formation under finite-state requirements.
  The framework begins not with time, space, objects, or probabilities, but with requirements such as finite representational capacity, single-state semantic stability, context-sensitive intervention, avoidance of explicit context labels, coherent world-formation, and intersubjective transformability. When these requirements cannot be realized within a single global Boolean event structure, the mismatch appears, under fixed-spacetime projection, as noncommutativity, interference, and quantum-like probability.
  Building on prior single-state approaches to contextuality, we reinterpret classical contextual bookkeeping cost as the fixed-spacetime shadow of contextual spacetime formation. Auxiliary memory or context labels in a classical representation correspond, in this account, to holonomy-like mismatch among locally Boolean logic-worlds. The interference term is the cross term generated when locally classical realization contributions are nontrivially glued and projected back into a fixed classical spacetime form.
  The result is a transcendental-operational realist account: objecthood, eventhood, probability, and spacetime are treated as forms of realization under requirements, while objectivity is defined by invariants preserved across observer- and history-dependent spacetime formations.

</details>


### [181] [Right-Sizing Communication and Recommendation Set Size in AI-Assisted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23944)
*Jing Dong,Prakirt Raj Jhunjhunwala,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究用户与AI推荐系统之间的交互，构建了贝叶斯框架，其中用户通过有成本且带噪声的消息传递偏好，AI据此推荐产品。在高维空间中，分析了两种采样策略下最优信息精度和推荐集大小的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统研究较少从信息论角度建模用户沟通成本与搜索成本之间的权衡，本文旨在理解在高维偏好空间中如何联合优化用户的沟通精度和AI的推荐数量。

Method: 采用互信息成本函数建模两类成本：沟通成本（随信息精度增加）和搜索成本（随推荐集大小增加）。在d维偏好空间下，考虑两种推荐采样分布：后验分布和优化倾斜分布，分别分析最优策略。

Result: 后验采样下存在混合机制，需要联合优化用户传递的信息量和AI推荐的物品数量；倾斜采样下最优策略仅使用沟通或搜索中成本较低的一种。

Conclusion: 不同推荐分布会显著影响最优交互策略的性质，后验采样需要协同优化，而倾斜采样可简化为单一维度决策。

Abstract: We model the interaction between a user and an AI driven recommendation system. The user initiates the process by conveying preference information through a costly and noisy message. The AI assistant, acting as a Bayesian agent, interprets the user's message to form a posterior belief about their true preferences and make product recommendations. In particular, it determines how many recommendations to present so as to maximize the user's expected utility from their final choice, while accounting for the search cost induced by the size of the recommendation set. We use mutual information based cost functions to model the two distinct costs incurred by the user during the interaction: (i) a communication cost, which increases with the precision of their preference message, and (ii) a search cost, which increases with the size of the recommendation set provided by the AI assistant.
  We study products and preferences which live in d dimensional space, and ask how the user's expected payoff can be maximized. For large d, we characterize how optimal message precision and recommendation set size depend on the cost parameters, under two distinct distributions from which recommendations can be sampled from the product universe: (i) Bayes' posterior belief, and (ii) an optimized tilted distribution. Under the posterior sampling scheme (i), we identify a hybrid regime, in which an efficient interaction policy requires jointly optimizing the amount of information (in bits) conveyed by the user and the number of recommendations provided by the AI assistant. In the tilted sampling scheme (ii), our results show that the optimal interaction policy uses only one of communication and search, favoring whichever of them is less costly.

</details>


### [182] [Accelerating Long-Tail Generation in Synchronous RLHF Training via Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23945)
*Long Zhao,Qinghe Wang,Jiaan Zhu,Youhui Bai,Zewen Jin,Chaoyi Ruan,Shengnan Wang,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAT，一种在RLHF生成阶段动态调整张量并行度的方法，通过预测引导的在线重配置和轻量级状态迁移机制，在LLaMA3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B上分别实现最高34.6%的生成延迟降低和27.2%的端到端训练迭代延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLHF三阶段流水线中，生成阶段因响应长度偏差导致有效批大小急剧缩小，GPU利用率低下；而主流框架采用静态张量并行配置，无法适应变化的batch特性，存在大量性能优化空间。

Method: PAT提出两种关键技术：1) 预测引导的在线重配置方法，基于离线分析决定重配置时机和目标TP配置，仅在预测延迟收益超过重配置开销时触发；2) 轻量级在线重配置机制，通过基于成本模型的KV缓存迁移与重计算选择、原地权重重分片以及缓存通信组复用来更新受TP变化影响的状态和布局。

Result: 在LLaMA3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B上使用DeepScaleR进行评估，PAT相比原始VeRL方案最高减少34.6%的生成延迟和27.2%的端到端RLHF训练迭代延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应TP配置能有效缓解RLHF生成阶段的资源利用瓶颈，显著提升训练效率，验证了动态调整并行策略在RLHF场景下的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a key post-training paradigm for improving model quality. However, the synchronous three-stage RLHF pipeline is often bottlenecked by the generation stage, where response-length skew causes the effective batch size to shrink rapidly during decoding, leaving GPUs underutilized while a few long responses remain unfinished. Mainstream frameworks employ a static tensor parallelism (TP) configuration that cannot adapt to changing batch characteristics, leaving substantial performance headroom unexplored. We propose PAT, an adaptive TP method that dynamically reconfigures TP during the generation stage of each RLHF iteration. PAT introduces two key techniques. First, a predictor-guided online reconfiguration method decides both the reconfiguration point and the target TP configuration based on offline profiling, triggering reconfiguration only when the predicted latency benefit outweighs the reconfiguration overhead. Second, a lightweight online reconfiguration mechanism updates only the states and layouts affected by TP changes: it adapts unfinished decoding states through a cost-model-based choice between KV-cache migration and recomputation, performs in-place weight resharding, and reuses cached communication groups. We implement PAT on top of SGLang and integrate it with the VeRL framework. Evaluations on LLaMA3.1-8B and Qwen3-14B using DeepScaleR show that PAT reduces generation latency by up to 34.6% and end-to-end RLHF training iteration latency by up to 27.2% compared to the original VeRL setup.

</details>


### [183] [Stop Comparing LLM Agents Without Disclosing the Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23950)
*Yunbei Zhang,Janet Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Weijie Xu,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K. Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI
